PREFACE TO 
SIXTH EDITION 
I started teaching astrology on 51h September 1998 with 
seven students. On the day students asked me to refer a 
book on the subject. It was hard for them to believe that 
there was not a single book on the subject available. This 
induced me to come up with the book "Accurate Predictive 
Methodology". Mr. Anoop Kumar showed his interest in the 
book and gave me his whole hearted support in coming up 
with the book. We went together to Mussorie, to write the 
book on 2"d February 1999 and completed the book in four 
days. The book did well in the market. The book was one of 
our prime tool to spread "Nadi Astrology" across the Globe. 
The book, "Accurate Predictive Methodology" was made 
available in three languages English, Hindi and Spanish. I 
am highly thankful to Shri Anoop Kumar, to my sister Smt. 
Prem Chaudhary and Maria Pillar Garcia Gil for having 
translated and assisting me in English, Hindi and Spanish 
languages. 
Today when I am writing these lines we are adding another 
school of "Nadi Astrology" in " Kalka Ji" Temple, Delhi. All 
these years we have been in touch with our readers and 
always took interest in listening to their suggestions. We 
have included all the suggestions and advises of our readers 
in the sixth edition of the book. The chapters in the book 
have been explained more. We have also added more 
illustrations for the benefit of the readers. 
I believe readers will appreciate efforts put in the book. I 
promise to come up with more research oriented books in 
the subject to make the subject more clear and 
comprehensive.

THUS SAID THE YOGI 
Astrology is not a question of belief. The scientific 
attitude one should take on any subject is whether it is 
true. The law of gravitation worked as efficiently as before 
Newton as after him. The cosmos would be fairly chaotic if 
its laws could not operate without the sanction of human 
belief. 
"Charlatans" have brought the ancient science to its 
present disrepute. Astrology is too vast, both mathemati­
cally and philosophically, to be rightly grasped except by 
men of profound understanding. If ignoramuses misread the 
heavens, and see there a scrawl instead of script, that is to 
be expected in this imperfect world. One should not dismiss 
the wisdom with the "wise". 
"All parts of creation are linked together and inter­
change their influences. The balanced rhythm of universe is 
rooted in reciprocity." Man , in his human aspect, has to 
combat two sets of admixture of earth, water, fire, air and 
ethereal elements; second the outer disintegrating powers, 
of nature. So long as man struggles with his morality, he is 
affected by the myriad mutations of heavens and earth. 
''Astrology is the study of man's response to plan­
etary stimuli. The stars have no conscious benevolence or 
animosity; they merely send forth positive and negative 
radiations. Of themselves, these do not help or harm hu­
manity, but offer a lawful channel for the outward opera­
tion of cause-effect equilibriums that each man has set 
into motion in the past. · 
A child is born on that day and at that hour when 
the celestial rays are in mathematical harmony with his indi­
vidual karma. His horoscope is a challenging portrait, reveal­
ing his unalterable past and its probable future results. But 
only men of intuitive wisdom can rightly interpret the natal 
chart: these are few. 
Paramhans Yoganand 
·-vii-· 
INTRODUCTION 
This book is my first humble attempt in the ancient field of 
astrology. Astrology over the years has undergone a sea 
change because of the continuous research being carried 
out by the scholars of astrology. There are different schools 
of thought in Astrology & not one school agrees with the 
principle of the other leading to doubts & confusions in the 
minds of the readers & the students. According to me, 
astrology has been a science, is a science & will be a science 
& therefore it should be studied in a scientific way & the 
latest research confirms this to be a pure science. 
There are two most important features of astrology, one 
predicting an event & the second timing of happening of 
the event. The entire happenings in the life of a Native are 
contained in the natal horoscope & the only thing required 
is correct application of the Astrology on it. The correct 
prediction presupposes the knowledge of exact time, date 
& place of birth. 
There is no intention to create or enter into any controversy 
about the system & methodology adopted by astrologers in 
prediction. It is a known fact that there is no actual division 
in "Zodiac" for dividing the same into various Signs & 
Nakshatras. It is only a system & methodology adopted to 
study the impact of the planets & stars on the native living 
on the planet "Earth" of the "Solar system". 
I have followed in this book the principles of "Nadi 
Astrology". According to the principles of the Nadi Astrology 
the Zodiac is divided into 12 equal sub divisions of signs 
with equal subdivisions of the 27 Nakshatras. These 
Nakshatras are further subdivided into unequal "Sub lords". 
The system lays greater emphasis on the sub lord which is 
stronger than the Nakshatra & in the same sequence the 
-1-
Nakshatra is stronger than the Planet. The experience shows 
that the system of study of Astrology through the Sign, 
Nakshatra & Sub lord gives most accurate results. In this 
system we cast the horoscope of the native by taking date, 
time & place of birth by drawing Lagan chart & Nirayana 
bhava chalit chart. The Lagan chart shows the planetary 
combinations of the planets in the Zodiac at the time of 
birth of the native whereas Nirayana bhava chalit shows 
the planetary combination of the Zodiac as seen by the 
native from that Latitude & Longitude with a correct 
"Ayanamsa". 
For fructification or culmination of an event, we don't have 
to see only one house relevant to fructification of event 
because other houses are also affected directly"or indirectly. 
The impact of the other houses is also to be taken into 
consideration while predicting an event. In addition to it, 
we have not to forget the conjunctions & aspects of the 
planets. 
Ultimately it has to be correlated to Vimshottari Dasa for 
timing the events. Timing of an event is the essence of 
Astrology & we can make it more precise by taking into 
account "Transits of the Planets". 
With this background I have tried to cover all the important 
events of life along with the variation in suc.h events & 
made this book exhaustive for the benefit of the readers & 
students of Ast~ology. 
In this book illustrations of natives have been given whose 
horoscopes I have studied & predictions have been made 
accordingly. Two horoscopes have been discussed in all the 
chapters to make the readers understand that the method 
is capable of handling all the events of the life of any native 
who had taken birth & will take birth on this planet Earth. 
Notable horoscopes of illustrious people have also been 
discussed in a separate chapter which substantiates the 
application of the system & method. 
Correction of the natal horoscope has also been made 
possible by applying Ruling planets & by matching all the 
past events of the native. 
-2-
Muhurat & Remedial measures in Astrology have been given 
in the separate chapter. Efficacy of remedial measures has 
to be judged by the Astrologers for which guidelines have 
been given. 
One last word for astrologers & readers, please apply 
common-sense & judge the General Standard of the 
horoscope before making any prediction. For example, if 
the combination of vehicle appears in the natal horoscope 
which is average, do not predict purchase of four wheeler 
because it will be a purchase of a two wheeler. Therefore, 
critical analysis of horoscopes is required before making 
any prediction depending on time, place & conditions. Though 
it is very difficult to concise all the events yet I have tried 
to cover all the aspects of human life based on my knowledge, 
observations & experiences. I appeal to all readers to write 
to me for any suggestions, improvements so that I 
incorporate the same in next edition of my book. 
Highness Heights, 
Mussorie 5 Feb. 99.

CHAPTER- 1 
CHARACTERISTICS 
OF PLANETS, SIGNS & 
HOUSES 
For the benefit of the readers, it is essential to write in brief 
the characteristics of Planets, Signs & Houses from the point 
of view of "Nadi Astrology" to be used in the book. 
Planets 
Lordship of the Planets: All the planets are lords of Signs 
except Rahu & Ketu. They are as follows: 
Planets Signs 
Sun Leo 
Moon Cancer 
Mars Aries, Scorpio 
Mercury Gemini, Virgo 
Jupiter Sagittarius, Pisces 
Saturn Capricorn, Aquarius 
Venus Taurus, Libra 
Uranus Capricorn 
Neptune Pisces 
Pluto Scorpio 
Aspects: All the planets Aspect various Signs from their 
Natal position in a Natal horoscope & in Transits. Following 
are the aspects of different planets which are practical : 
-4-· 
Planets Aspects on various Signs 
Sun 7 
Moon 7 
Mercury 7 
Venus 7 
Pluto 7 
Uranus 7 
Neptune 7 
Mars 7,4,8 
Jupiter 7,5,9 
Saturn 7,3,10 
Rahu 5,9 
Ketu 5,9 
Natural Malefic: Natural malefic planets are Rahu, Ketu, 
Saturn, Mars, Uranus, Neptune & Pluto. 
Natural Benefic: Natural benefic planets are Jupiter, Venus, 
Mercury, Moon & Sun. Moon is always a benefic while 
decreasing & increasing. It should be considered a benefic 
during "Amavasya" also. In other words Moon is always a 
benefic. Similarly there should not be any confusion about 
Mercury. It is also a benefic always. Sun should also be 
considered a benefic since it signifies medicine, government 
& father etc. 
Separative planets: Separative tendencies are signified by 
Sun, Saturn, Rahu & Ketu. 
Natural Strength: Rahu, Ketu, Saturn & Mars are strong 
planets. They signify the events predominantly in their DBA 
& Transits than Sun, Moon, Mercury, Venus & Jupiter. Uranus, 
Neptune, & Pluto are also very strong. They do not have 
any role in any DBA but their presence is felt certainly in 
Transits & behaviour of the native. 
-5-
Friendship & Enmity: There is no Friendship or Enmity 
between any of the planets. All planets work in the manner 
explained in the book. 
In India most of the Astrologers are not using Uranus, Neptune 
& Pluto because Ancient Savants of Astrology have not 
written anything on it. It is a well known fact that all the 
three planets have been discovered recently. But since all 
of them are heavenly bodies & that too within our "Solar 
system", they must have been affecting the life on "Earth". 
Therefore I find it essential to write a few words on these 
planets. All of them do not take part in the DBA therefore 
they largely do not affect the occurring & the timing of the 
event. Their presence is felt in Transits & on the behaviour 
of the Native.Uranus, Neptune and Pluto are transpersonal 
planets used much in Western Astrology. According to 
Western Astrology if these planets aspect Sun, Moon, 
Mercury, Venus and Mars then the native is very dynamic 
and intelligent. 
Uranus: Uranus signifies revolutions, rebellions, anarchy, 
rioting i.e. sudden blows & events in a Native's life. It rules 
Capricorn Sign. Uranus shares lordship with Saturn. According· 
to Western Astrology Uranus is lord of Aquarius whereas 
according to Indian Astrology Uranus is lord of Capricorn. 
Uranus represents revolutions hence is also associated to 
freedom. The change in the life of a native will be unexpected, 
drastic and not wished for. The new change will be like the 
other side of the coin. It also rules intuition, creativity, 
independent ideas and leadership qualities. 
Neptune: Neptune is psychic in behaviour. It develops 
confusions in the mind of Native. It signifies cheats, 
deceivers, forgers, kidnapping etc. According to both Indian 
and western Astrology Neptune is lord of Pisces. It represents 
sacrifices, love, sacrifice for love and fusing with others. It 
also rules confusions and victims of the situation however it · 
is in our hand to overpower and weaken others. It also 
creates addiction to tobacco or alcohol and the reason can 
be result misunderstanding in love. It also rules spiritualism 
and mysticism. 
Pluto: Pluto is a kin of Mars but it signifies group activity. It 
signifies dacoits, massacres, gangsters, mass tragedies, 
-6-
racketeers etc. Pluto rules Scorpio according to both western 
and Indian Astrology. It signifies death like transformation. 
Sometimes beloved people separate from our lives and the 
cause of seperation can be death. Sometimes they will come 
back to us but the relations will not be the same again as 
they have transformed. It also gives us the opportunity to 
modify ourselves. Many fears are experienced in this path 
and in the end it is total liberation from the responsibilities.

CHAPTER- 2 
RULES OF NADI ASTROLOGY 
It is a universal fact that there is ·a Zodiac & there are 
twelve planets in solar system ( fort he purpose of astrology 
we are considering all Sun, Moon, Rahu & Ketu as planets) 
with 27 Nakshatras which are considered relevant for 
astrology. Without going into any controversy about the 
system & methodology adopted by others, I will presume a 
minimum basic knowledge of astrology by the reader. 
Ayanamsa 
The first and foremost important factor before casting a 
Horoscope is to take the correct "Ayanamsa". Ayanamsa is 
the rate at which the point of intersection of the Ecliptic & 
the Celestial equator moves in retrograde motion. The correct 
Ayanamsa is given by the figure 50.2388475". Reader should 
know that the fixed zodiac & the Sayana zodiac were in the 
same degree in the year 291 A.D. 
The astrologer should proceed now to cast the horoscope 
of the native by using the correct Ayanamsa. For casting 
the horoscope help can be taken from the KP. Ephemeris or 
by Lahiri Ephemeris. Astrologers can take the help of correct 
software too available in the market. It is worth noting that 
KP. & Lahiri Ephemeris are taking the same Ayanamsa but 
Lahiri had taken 285 A.D. as the year of coinciding of both 
the zodiacs. Astrologers should add 6' to Ascendant and all 
the positions of the planets in case they are using Lahiri 
Ayanamsa. 
Nirayana Bhava Chalit 
In addition to the Ascendant horoscope, we also have to 
prepare the "Nirayana Bhav Chalit Chart". The bhava Madhya 
of the Ascendant chart is considered in Nirayana Bhava Chalit 
- 8 -
as starting point of the Bhava or House. For casting "Nirayana 
Bhav Chalit" Chart "Raphael's Tables of Houses" is 
recommended. A representative horoscope has been given 
below of the native born on 7th June 1950 at 22.30 hrs. 
Delhi Lat. 28° 40' N Long. 7r 13' E.

Sub -lord 
Nadi Astrology is based on Nakshatra & its sub divisions 
called "Sub lords". There are nine inequal sub lords in each 
Nakshatra. The sub lords are divided on the basis of Vimsottari 
Dasa & are given by the following formula 
Years of Planet Vim.Dasa X Nakshatra 
subdivision 
Sub-Lord 
Total years in Vim. Dasa 
First Nakshatra in Zodiac is of Ashwini ruled by Ketu. First 
Sub-lord in the Nakshatra Ashwini is Ketu ; second Venus 
;third Sun according to Vimshottari Dasa & so on upto the 
9th & the last i.e. Mercury. Similarly the next Nakshatra fs 
Bharani ruled by Venus. The first Sub lord will automatically 
be Venus & the last Ketu. Now we'll go on to the mathematical 
calculation of the Sub lords. We all know that each Nakshatra 
is of 13° 20 'or 800 ·.According to the formula given above 
the first Sub lord of Ketu will be of 
7 X 800 ' = 46 '40 "

A table of all the Sub lords in each Nakshatra is given in the 
Annexure. 
Aspects & Conjunctions 
Aspects & Conjunctions of all the planets are to be seen 
from sign to sign. In the other words, aspects & conjunctions 
are to be seen from the "Lagna Chart". There is a difference 
in the case of Rahu & Ketu. They are capable to exert their 
influence on the planets in the next sign which are up to r 
apart. 
- 12-
Position & Lordships of the Planets 
Position & Lordships of the planets are to be seen in "Nirayana 
Bhav Chalit" chart. 
Rules of Nadi Astrology 
' 
Rule -1 
12th house to every house mars the significance of that 
house. 
12th from Ascendant is the 12th House. Ascendant is body 
and 12th House signifies death. 
l}th to 12th i.e. 11th will stop the expenses & losses hence 
signifies profit. 
whereas 12th from 1oth i.e. gth will separate one from 
the job. 
& 12th from gth i.e. 8th will create obstacles in 
the life. 
& 12th from ih i.e. 6th will separate from the 
marital home & may lead to divorce. 
but 12th from 6th i.e. 5th will make one free from 
disease & litigation although the person may 
not be inclined to work. 
12th from sth i.e. 4th will deny the children. 
12th from 4th i.e. 3'd will be loss of property or 
denying the purchase of property & native 
will go away from the house I motherland. 
There are certain exceptions to it as follows : 
12th from 11th i.e. 10th is good for business as both are 
complementary houses here. 
Similarly 12th from 5th i.e. 4th is good education whereas 5th is 
house of intelligence. 
and 12th from 4th i.e. 3'd is good in regard to vehicles since 
3'd House is the House of Travel. 
- 13 -
The above rule clearly indicates that if a penultimate house 
is signified by a planet with the main house then there is 
question mark on the fructification of the results of the 
houses by the planet in its DBA. However if the main house 
is signified by the planet & the penultimate house by the 
Nakshatra & in the same way the main house is signified; by 
the Nakshatra & penultimate house by the Sub lord then 
significance of the house is completely destroyed. In case 
the penultimate house is signified by the Planet or the 
Nakshatra & the main house by the Nakshatra or the planet 
respectively the significance the house remains in intact. 
Similarly complete combinations of negation deny the events 
in DBA are explained in detail in each chapter. 
Rule- 2 
In elongation to the above rule 2nd house to every house 
enhances the significances of the penultimate house. 
Rule- 3 
According to Nadi Astrology a Planet in a Horoscope is under 
the influence of the following three factors. It is desired to 
give its ·results in DBA keeping in view these factors. 
1) Its position & the lordship of houses in the horoscope. 
2) Its Nakshatra. 
3) Its Sub lord. 
According to Nadi Astrology, Sub lord is stronger than 
the Nakshatra Lord & in the same sequence Nakshatra 
Lord is stronger than the Planet. In other words planet is 
weaker than the Nakshatra Lord & Nakshatra Lord is weaker 
than the Sub lord. 
Extension to the rule is that if Sub Lord and Nakshatra both 
signify opposite combinations then the strength of Nakshatra 
and Sub Lord becomes the same. The rule is discussed in 
detail in the next page under the heading "Strength of the 
planets". 
- 14 -
Rule- 4 
There are various Dasa systems available in Indian Astrology. 
I recommend "Vimsottari Dasa". According to Nadi 
Astrology in Vimsottari dasa, Dasa lord (Mahadasa Lord) 
is. the strongest . It is stronger than the Bhukti lord 
(An tar Dasha lord) & in the same sequence Bhukti lord 
is stronger than the Antar Dasha lord (Pratayantar 
Dasha lord). In all the chapters I'll be referring Dasa, Bhukti, 
Antar as DBA. 
Rule -5 
Single House does not give any result. Few Houses join 
together to fructify an event in the life of a native. For 
··example if we talk the results of any House, say the 1 ih 
House then 1 ih House signifies a number of events. Million 
Dollar question is do all the events will happen in the life of 
native or any one of the events will happen in the life? 
Strength of a Planet 
By the strength of a planet, we mean the authority of the 
planet on the fructification or culmination of an event in the 
DBA 
Strength of a planet is delineated by the strength of its 
Planet, Nakshatra & Sub lord. If Sub lord signifies full 
combination of the event & Nakshatra & Planet endorse it 
i.e. also signify the same combination, the event is going to 
happen surely. Such a Planet is said to be the strongest for 
culmination of the particular event. 
On the other hand if Sub lord signifies the full combination of 
culmination of an event & the Nakshatra & the Planet negate 
then the event becomes nearly impossible to happen. 
In other case, if the Nakshatra & Planet signify the full 
combinations but Sub lord negates, the event can happen 
in the end. The planet is rendered weak here. 
If planet and the Nakshatra does not negate an event and 
support the Sub lord &the Sub lord signifies full combinations 
the Planet here will be in full strength to fructify the event. 
- 15 -
There can be a case when all the Planet, Nakshatra & the 
Sub lord signifying only facilitator houses/ combinations in 
such a case the Planet will have to look on other DBA planets. 
If other DBA planets will be strong then the event will take 
place otherwise not. We can say that the planet's strength 
here is ordinary. 
Lastly if Planet & the Nakshatra show the combinations of 
fructification of event & the Sub lord signify facilitator houses 
the Planet here also has full strength for culmination of the 
event in DBA. 
I'll like to add here that Natural strength of the planets 
should also be kept in mind here. For the same combinations 
the results of Rahu, Ketu, Saturn will come more profoundly 
followed by Mars & other Natural benefic Sun, Moon, Jupiter, ~ 
Mercury & Venus. 
This is how we'll have to judge the strength of the planets & 
nothing else has to be kept in mind in this regard. 
Cusp 
Starting degree of the house is called the Cusp or the 
Cuspal degree of the House in "Nadi Astrology". The Sub 
lord at the Cuspal degree is called the "Cuspal Sub lord". 
Cuspal Sub Lord has its own importance. The strength of 
the House is drawn from the strength of the Cuspal Sub 
Lord. If the Cuspal Sub lord signifies the combinations 
connected with the House the House is strong. The 
concerned events will take place in the concerned DBA. If 
the Sub lord negates the event the event will not take 
place even in the DBA promising the event strongly. For 
example, if the Cuspal Sub lord of the 4th house if signifies 4 
or 11 or good houses 2,9 in this regard the property will be 
purchased by the individual in the DBA of 4,11, 12. On the 
other hand if Cuspal Sub lord signifies houses 3,5,6,8,12 , 
the DBA of 4,11 won't work & i.e. there will not be purchase 
of any property rather the property inherited by the native 
from his forefathers will also be endangered in the bad DBA. 
Results of Planets 
To know the results of the Planets we'll pen down the

following: 
1) The Lordship & the position of all the Planets. 
2) The Nakshatras & the Sub lords. of all the planets.

Lordship & the position of the planets. 
1) Write down the position of the planet by looking its 
position in the "Nirayana Bhav Chalit". 
2) Write down the Lordship of the planet by looking the 
lordships in the "Nirayana Bhav Chalit".

with.

aspected by.

they are posited.

1) None

Timing of an event 
As discussed above, Dasa lord is stronger than the Bhukti 
lord & Bhukti lord is stronger than the Antra lord. Hence if 
Dasa Lord allows an event according to the combinations 
given in the following chapters the event will take place in 
the Dasa. To further pin point the event we'll see the strong 
Bhukti lord which signifies the same. The event will take 
place in that Bhukti, however if Dasa lord is very strong the 
- 19 -
event can take place in its Bhukti also. Now as the Bhuktis 
are from several months to years, to pin point the event 
further we'll have to choose a strong Antar which will time 
the event. 
In majority of the cases the Antra Dasa elongates to several 
months & the astrologer I native is interested to know the 
date of fructification of the event for that Transits of the 
Planets are studied. 
There can be cases that the Dasa lord does not allow the 
event. In such a case we'll straightaway go to the next 
Dasa which allows the event. Similarly if Bhukti is not allowing 
the event, the next Bhukti is to be seen. 
Transits of Planets 
Transits of the planets are to be seen to pin point the events 
precisely. It is relevant to see the Transits when the DBA 
have endorsed a particular event. 
First of all see the transits of any of slower moving planets 
i.e. either of Saturn, Jupiter, Rahu or Ketu. They should 
transit the relevant houses that are available in the 
combination Then go on to see the transit of fast moving 
planets viz. Sun, Mercury, Venus and Mars. When they join 
the slower moving planets, the event takes place. Care should 
be taken that the transits of only those planets have to be 
seen which signify the event. 
Following are position of the planets in the "Transits" which 
help an astrologer to pin point the event with marked 
precision for the benefit of the native. 
Any of the three planets which signify the event may I may 
not be from the DBA, should 
conjoin in one house or 
mutually aspect each other or 
transit the significator Houses or 
aspect them or 
transit over their natal positions 
- 20-
Event will take place on the day when 
A transiting planet signifying event either transits 
exactly over Natal degree of planet signifying the 
event. 
or two planets signifying event transiting in a sign 
conjunct within one degree. 
or any planet signifying event transits exactly over 
cuspal degree of a House which signifies event. 
or Moon transits over the three conjoined planets 
signifying event. Note that Moon should also signify 
event otherwise its Transit is not relevant . 
or the transit of the Antar lord in the favourable 
Nakshatra. Favourable Nakshatra here means 
Nakshatra of a Planet that signifies event.

CHAPTER- 3 
EDUCATION 
Education means acquiring information & knowledge through 
parents, teachers & gurus. This information & knowledge is 
available in books, texts, periodicals & scriptures & is taught 
through schools, colleges & educational institutions. 
The information & knowledge so acquired when applied in 
various fields of life becomes the wisdom. Wisdom is 
application of information & knowledge at appropriate time 
place & conditions. 
Houses 
Astrologically, houses which signify information & knowledge 
acquired in schools, colleges & educational institutions are: 
2"d House: 2nd House signifies the knowledge acquired. 
4th House: 4
1
h House is the prime house of education and it 
also signifies primary education. 
gth House: gth House signifies higher education. 
11th House: 11th House signifies gain in education. 
In addition to the above houses of education there are also 
certain facilitator houses, they are : 
5th House: 5th house signifies intelligence & the 
comprehension of the native. 
3'd House: 3rd house is the prime house of written work, 
communication & printing. 
11th house in astrology signifies gain when it gets connected 
- 22 -
with the relevant houses of education it combines to give 
gain of the relevant house. In such cases it also enhances 
the significance of house to heights. 
Planets 
In addition to the houses which signify education there are 
planets which also signify education, they are: 
Mercury: It signifies analytical power, intelligence & 
communication skill. 
Jupiter: It signifies wisdom & comprehension. 
Combinations 
The system of education is regulated through the examination 
system where the appraisal of the native's performance is 
carried out by allocation of the grades & percentage of 
marks so obtained. In line with the modern day education 
the following combinations in the DBA planets give the 
respective grades & percentage of marks. 
4,9,11

Failures & obstacles 
When 6,8, 12 gets connected with any of the combinations 
mentioned above except 4,9, 11 or 4,11 or 5,11 will result 
into compartment in the particular subject held during the 
relevant DBA. If 8,12 is signified with either 4,9, 11 or 4,11; 
5,11 child has scientific bent of mind. 
No Education: When 6,8, 12 are repeated in the DBA in the 
native's horoscope with the malefics in the 4th house it 
results into no education for the native. Alternatively it can 
be predicted that the native is an "illiterate". 
No inclination for Education: We also come across natives 
in peculiar circumstances when the native belongs to well 
- 23 -
to do family where native is provided with all the means of 
education but the native has no inclination for education. 
For such cases the combination 3,6,8 or 3,6, 12 or 3,8, 12 
is profound in the horoscope. 
Foreign Education: In the modern world where education 
is desired by all, sometimes due to compelling circumstances 
of parents, lack of facility in the native's place, transfer or 
otherwise the native has to leave his home for having his 
education . Since education away from native's place has 
inherent foreign travel it is desirable that foreign education 
is seen under the chapter "Foreign Travels". 
Scholarships: To promote healthy competition amongst the 
students the concept of scholarship to the meritorious 
candidates is prevalent in our educational institutions. It 
gives a sense of pride to the student, parents & other 
relatives. 
In astrology scholarship is signified by 6th house & for 
gaining the scholarship 11th house must get connected 
therefore getting the scholarship 6 & 11 houses in a 
native's horoscope should get profound in the DBA periods. 
Success in Competitive Examinations & Interviews: 
Admission to good course or prestigious institutions is 
regulated through competitive examinations, for success in 
such competitive examinations are achieved by the native 
when the DBA planets signify houses 6,11 or 4, 9, 11 . In few 
cases where the seats available are more then the 
combinations of 4,11 or 5,11 in present DBA also results 
into success. 
Furthermore, the competitive written examinations are 
followed by interviews or group discussions or both. The 
interview or group discussion requires a person to have 
good communication & vocal skills which in astrology are 
signified by the 3rd house. A native having combination of 
3,11 or 3,6, 11 in present DBA gets through interviews & 
group discussions. 
Field of Education: The most relevant & important question 
asked by the native or the parents is about the branch or 
- 24 -
discipline the native is going to pursue for higher education. 
Normally a native who has scored good grades & is offered 
different studies like science, arts, commerce, Engg., 
medical, journalism etc. he is not able to finalise the studies 
he should pursue. At these junctions astrology becomes a 
great help in identifying the natives strength in pursuing a 
particular branch or discipline of study. 
If the native is having combination of education in majority 
of planets he will remain connected to education throughout 
life. The native can pursue teaching as profession. 
If the native is having combination of disease 1 ,6,8, 12 with 
that of good houses of education in majority of planets 
then he can pursue medical profession. 
Houses 8,12 with combinations of good education viz. 
4,5,9,11 in majority of planets indicates that native should 
pursue engineering. 
If 4,11 is signified in majority of planets child can opt for 
civil engineering, automobile engineering, architecture, 
interior decoration and allied fields. 
lfthe native is having combination of6, 10,11 in majority of 
planets he can opt for business studies as well as can appear 
for administrative services. 
2,11 along with good houses of education indicates 
commerce, CA's etc. 
Combination of 7 ,8, 12 along with good houses of education 
and 10,11 is suitable for military sciences, security etc. 
Combination of 3,11 promotes journalism. 
Combination of 6,8, 12 with good Houses of education 
promotes study of law. 
The above combination should be judiciously applied by an 
astrologer in the Horoscope of a native e.g. in the Horoscope 
of an MBBS doctor majority of planets signify 10,11 then he 
can be advised to do post graduation in Hospital

the govt. whereas Jupiter or Venus confirms the prize or 
award from non govt. institutions & authorities.

CHAPTER4 
LITIGATION 
These days man has become such an egoist, corrupt, 
manipulative & embroiled in various controversies that you 
hardly come across any person who has not faced litigation 
whether by initiating himself or somebody else initiating 
against him including the government & other agencies. 
Litigation as commonly understood means any dispute or 
difference or disagreement over anything which results into 
the native approaching the courts, tribunal or any other 
competent authority. 
Litigation also comes into play when there is violation, 
infringement of any right, title or interest of any kind. Broadly 
litigation can be categorised as follows: 
1) Commercial 
2) Non Commercial 
3) Social 
4) Others 
Commercial litigation means violation of contractual obligation 
or non payment of dues or taxes, cesses, levies & impositions 
of any kind. 
Commercial litigation for a layman means legal case emanating 
out of trade, commerce, industry or business. 
Non Commercial litigation includes the infringement of rights 
& the damages on account of physical & mental injury. 
Social litigation means & includes the family disputes, loss 
of reputation, harassment & mental cruelty. 
Any kind of litigation not covered under the above mentioned 
three falls into the other categories. 
- 34-
Houses 
61h House: In astrology 6th House is prime house of litigation 
by the native or against the native. 
81h House: 8th House plays significant role in litigation as it 
signifies tensions, obstacles, failures & insults. 
121h House: Similarly 1 ih House involves upon expenses in 
litigation, payment of penalties, dues or taxes & loss of 
sleep, imprisonment & secret plots of enemies. It is also 
prime house of loss. 
Planets 
All the first rate natural malefic planets Rahu, Ketu and 
Saturn promote litigations by the native or against the native. 
They also promote disputes, tensions, conviction by the 
court, imprisonment, kidnapping, insults, impositions of 
penalties, dues or taxes etc. 
Combinations & Timing of Litigation 
Astrologically if all or any of the 6,8, 12 houses are signified 
in the DBA of natural malefic planets Rahu, Ketu or Saturn 
native is involved in litigation. If Rahu, Ketu or Saturn 
conjunct with or aspect the DBA planets signifying 6,8, 12 
then also native gets involved in litigation. Kindly note that 
any of the 6, 8 or 12 house alone in the planet, Nakshatra 
or Sub lord of DBA planets doesn't result into litigation. It is 
only when any two or all of the above houses get connected 
in the planet, Nakshatra or the Sub lord of DBA of natural 
malefic there is danger of litigation, damages & penalties to 
the native. If any of the 6, 8 or 12 are repeated in the 
planet & Nakshatra or Nakshatra & the Sub lord in DBA of 
the natural malefic then also penalties are imposed on the 
native. 
In all such cases if Sun or Moon also get involved in the 
DBA the native suffers at the hands of the state government, 
central government, Municipal corporation & other 
government authorities. 
- 35-

Nakshatra or Planet alone with good Houses therefore 
litigation will not come into pass in their periods. To conclude 
Litigation will not take place if 6 or 8 or 12 alone is signified 
in any of the Planet, Nakshatra or Sub-lord. 
Litigation will not take place in the planets which signify 6 
or 8 or 12 repeatedly in Planet, Nakshatra or Nakshatra and

CHAPlER- 5 
PROPERTY & VEHICLE 
The most common question an astrologer faces from the 
native is about acquisition of property or vehicle because it 
is distant dream of majority of persons specially in 
metropolitan cities. Property purchase involves the entire 
life savings of an individual. It is also correlated with the 
status of the family. 
Purchase of Property 
Houses 
Houses involved in purchase of property are 
4th House Prime house of property. 
11th House Gain of property & fulfillment of desires. 
12th House Expense incurred during the purchase of 
property. 
Planets 
Natural significators of property are Mars & I or Saturn . 
No property can be purchased or sold without their 
involvement in DBA or they should aspect or conjuct DBA 
planets. They also play major role in transits during purchase 
or sale of properties. 
Combination & Timing of Purchase of Property 
Native purchases a property when the DBA lords signify the 
combination of 4,11,12. Any of the DBA lord should be 
Mars or Saturn or DBA lords should conjunct or aspected by 
Mars or Saturn. After the DBA has signified purchase of 
property Transits of planets will exactly pin point purchase 
of property. 
- 49-
Purchase through Loan 
Since purchase of property involves huge amount a native 
in some case takes loan through banks, financial institutions 
& others. In such cases there is involvement of 6th house 
with the combination of purchase of property in DBA. So the 
full combination of purchase of property through loan is 
4,6, 11,12 & the significator planets Mars & I or Saturn. 
Property is also purchased or acquired through legacy, dowry 
& paying through the savings of provident funds or insurance. 
If the property is purchased through the savings of provident 
funds or insurance 8th house is signified with the combination 
of purchase of property. The full combination of purchase of 
property in this case is 4,8, 11,12 Mars&/ or Saturn Now if 
the property is acquired through legacy the 8th house is 
repeated with 4,11 houses. Now one thing should be carefully 
noted here that the involvement of 12th house will not be 
there since no expense is made by the native in this regard. 
Similarly in acquiring property from dowry same combination 
would appear during the marriage of the native (see the 
chapter of marriage also in this regard). 
Purchase in joint names 
Property is purchased in joint names when House 3 is signified 
with combination of purchase of property. House 3 is 12th 
from 4th hence it restricts native to become the landlord of 
whole piece of land or property. 
Purchase in instalments 
When 4,12 is signified in the DBA the property is purchased 
in instalments. This case is a little different from the purchase 
through loan. Here no loan is taken &the property is acquired 
after a long process of payments in parts. 
Location & Status of Property 
If natural benefics Sun, Moon, Mercury, Venus & Jupiter 
arein DBA planets the property purchased is in good & prime 
location. If somehow Sun & Moon are predominantly signified, 
property is purchased from Govt. or Govt. authority. There 
is an expansion in property in DBA of Jupiter or the property 
- 50 -
is huge. Venus signifies prime, sophisticated & beautiful 
property. It also signifies interior decoration. However if the 
property is purchased in the DBA of natural malefics Rahu, 
Saturn or Ketu the property is purchased in bad location. If 
somehow Saturn is predominant in DBA with other natural 
malefics, the property purchased is also smaller than the 
needs of the native. 
Commercial property 
Commercial property is signified by Mercury & the 7th house. 
The commercial property will be purchased when Mercury is 
any of the DBA planet & 7th house is signified with the 
combination of property purchase as discussed above. 
Construction of Property 
If a plot of land is purchased & the native wants to commence 
the construction at the same stage or the later stage the 
same combination of purchase of property should appear in 
DBA during construction. 
Rental Income 
Native will earn rental income in the DBA of 4,6, 11. The 
tenant will not vacate the property if the combination of 
loss of property ( given below) is signified in the subsequent 
DBA of the native. The property is revived in litigation when 
the combination of 4,6, 11 is revived in the subsequent DBA 
If the combination doesn't reappear in DBA the native has 
to loose the property forever. 
Loss of Property 
Loss of property can be due to various reasons like losing in 
litigation, Mafia, attachments etc. Loss of property takes 
place in the DBA of malefics signifying 3,6,8, 12. Demolition 
of property by the Govt. authority will take place in the DBA 
of Sun or Moon . The combination 4,8, 12 will be signified by 
the DBA planets. 
Partition of Property 
Partition of property will take place between two or more 
parties when 3,11 will be signified in the DBA of separative 
planets. 
- 51 -
Common question asked by the native is whether the property 
purchased by him is lucky or not. If combination of purchase 
of property is signified in the DBA of natural benefics with 
5,9, 10, 11 or 5,9, 10, 11 are signified in the DBA of natural 
benefics the person will be happy in life & satisfied. On the 
other hand if 6,8, 12 are signified in the DBA of natural malefics 
during purchase of property& in subsequent DBA the person 
will have to face series of troubles in life therefore he will 
believe the property is not lucky for him. The astrologer in 
all such cases shall deal with the native in a very articulate 
manner. 
Sale of Property 
Sale of property is signified by the combination 3,5,10.Since 
5,10 of the native are 4,11 to the opposite party & 3 being 
12th from the 4th signifies loosing the possession of the 
property by the native. Here also the significator planets 
remain the same i.e. Mars & Saturn. Therefore native sells 
property in the DBA of any of Mars or Saturn signifying the 
combination of 3,5, 10. After DBA planet have signified sale 
of property transits of planets will exactly pin point the sale 
of property. 
If somehow 11 is signified by the DBA with the combination 
of sale of property the native is able to sell the property at 
higher than the market price. On the other hand if same 
combination is signified with 6,8, 12 in the DBA the property 
is sold at lower than the market price. 
If both 3,5, 10 & 4,11 are signified in the DBA of the native 
there is simultaneous sale & purchase of the property by 
the native. 
Change of Residence 
Change of place of residence of a native is signified by the 
combination 3,5. They should be signified in the DBA planets 
of separatives at the particular time. 
Purchase of Vehicle 
Purchase of vehicle is a common phenomenon because of 
the fast life & the distances involved. Transits of planets will 
exactly pin point purchase of vehicle.

Houses ·

property.

Facilitator house

travel & leaving the home for various reasons such as 
business, service, tourism etc. In case of vehicle purchase 
it is a facilitator house whereas in property being 1 ih from 
the 4th it restricts the purchase of property. 
Planet 
Venus : Venus is the significator planet in both sale & 
purchase of vehicles. 
Combinations & Timing of Purchase of Vehicle 
When an astrologer has to predict the purchase of vehicle

Any of the DBA planet should be Venus or conjuct or aspected

Purchase of Vehicle through Loan

Theft of Vehicle

vehicle is never recovered. 
There are few cases when the vehicle is set on fire and 
sometimes vehicle catches fire due to fault in the vehicle.

Non Repayment of loan & loss of Vehicle

of 4,6, 11 appears in the subsequent DBA . 
Maintenance of vehicle 
Vehicle is kept & maintained in excellent condition when DBA 
of benefics run signifying good houses 5,9,2,3,4, 11,1 0. If 
6,8, 12 are signified in DBA of malefics the vehicle is maintained 
in bad condition. In addition it also denotes huge expenses 
& problems in the vehicles. 
Sale of Vehicle 
Sale of vehicle is signified by the combination 3,5,10.Since 
5,10 of the native are 4,11 to the opposite party & 3 being 
12th from the 4th signifies parting the vehicle by the native. 
Here also the significator planet remain the same i.e. Venus. 
Therefore native sells vehicle in the DBA of Venus signifying 
the combination of 3,5, 10. 
If somehow 11 is signified by the DBA with the combination 
of sale of vehicle the native is able to sell the vehicle at 
higher than the market price.On the other hand if same 
combination is signified with 6,8, 12 in the DBA the vehicle is 
sold at lower than the market price. 
If both 3,5, 10 & 4,11 are signified in the DBA of the native 
there is simultaneous sale & purchase of the vehicle by the 
native .Transits of planets will exactly pin point sale of vehicle. 
General

machinery in factories, business establishments, home 
appliances, electronic machinery & computers etc. To give 
an example, computers, TV., V.C.R. A.C. are signified by 
Venus whereas manufacturing machines are signified by 
Jupiter & washing machines come under the portfolio of Moon.

CHAPTER-6 
HEALTH 
Healthy body has a healthy mind. This maxim has universal 
application because once health is lost everything is lost. A 
sick body can not achieve success in any of the fields which 
have already been discussed in this book. 
Medical science is a very vast field where a continuous 
research is going on for finding the remedial measure for 
curing any type of sickness. I shall only be dealing in this 
chapter the important aspect of medical science & the 
application of the astrology on the same. 
In astrology human body has been divided into 12 parts & 
they have been correlated with 12 houses as follows : 
Houses Body Parts 
1 Head 
2 Face, Eyes, Right eye of male & left eye of the 
female, mouth, tongue, upper food pipe, teeth 
3 Ears, lower food pipe, wind pipe, Arms 
4 Lungs, Heart 
5 Heart, Stomach, Spleen, Pancreas, Liver 
6 Small intestine 
7 Sexual organs (internal), Kidneys 
8 Sexual organs (external), Large intestine, Anus 
9 Hips, Hip joints 
10 Legs, upper part 
11 Legs, lower part 
12 Feet, Left eye of the male & Right eye of the 
female 
Similarly planets have significant role in the general health 
- 65 -
of the native & characteristics of various planets from the 
view of medical astrology has been given below. 
Sun 
It is bilious in temperament. It rules over heart, head, brain, 
bones, right eye, spleen, lungs & throat. Diseases signified 
by sun are sun stroke, heart trouble, headache, blood 
pressure, scorches, sharp fever, baldness, weak eye sight & 
eye diseases, hyper-irritability, diseases arising from 
inflammation of bile, stomach ailments etc. 
Moon 
It has phlegmatic temprament. It rules over blood, all juices 
& enzymes in the body including milk, stomach, breast of 
ladies, mind, left eye. It rules over menstrural cycles. Diseases 
signified by Moon are cold & cold fever, dysentry, vomitting, 
eye diseases, epilepsy, throat trouble, phlegmatic disorders, 
dropsy, dyspespia, Deri beri, colic pains, asthma, blood 
poisoning, menstural disorders, diseases of the female 
reproductive system, mental aberrations, nervousness, 
emotions etc. 
Mars 
It has a bilious temprament. It rules over ear, nose, forehead, 
sinewsfibre, muscular tissues, gall bladder, prostate glands, 
uterus, external sex organs, testicles, haemoglobin, tissues 
in the body. Diseases signified by Mars are fever, small pox, 
chicken pox, inflammations, high blood pressure, blood 
disorders, hernia, fistula, Meningitis (braifever), itching, 
miscarriages, excessive bleeding, mental aberrations etc. 
Mars also represents accidents, surgical operations & 
surgeons .. 
Mercury 
Mercury has all the three tempraments viz. wind, bile, phlegm. 
In a human body Mercury represents skin, brain, nervous 
system, abdomen, tongue, lungs, throat, nose etc.Diseases 
signified by Mercury are skin diseases, mental weakness, 
nervous breakdown, impotency, ear diseases including 
deafness, stammering, palpitation, leucoderma, mental 
aberration. 
- 66 -
Jupiter 
Jupiter is phlegmatic. Jupiter is the only planet which protects 
against diseases but once it signfies 6,8, 12 houses it causes 
deadliest diseases such as diabetes & cancer. It also signifies 
liver disorders, gall bladder disease, splenic disease, ear 
troubles,jaundice, vertigo, obesity etc. It rules over liver, 
flesh, ear, pancreas, spleen in the body. 
Venus 
Venus is windy & phlegmatic. This most beautiful & 
sophisticated planet gives worst diseases of sexual organs 
(venereal diseases) such as gonorrhea, leucoderma &syphilis. 
It also rules over eye diseases, disease of tear glands, 
eczema, mucous disease, kidney problems &faliures. In the 
body of a human being it rules over reproductive organs, 
sexual drive, chin, cheeks, kidneys etc. 
Saturn 
Saturn has windy & phlegmatic temprament. It rules over 
legs, feet, teeth, bones, nails, hair, secretive system. It is 
the prime planet which rules over diseases, their chronicity 
& incurability. Diseases caused by it are either chronic or 
incurable. Its special portfolios are paralysis, insanity, 
rheumatism, heart pain & heart attack, fatigue, depression, 
phobias,deformation of bones, cancers & elephantisis. It is 
also prime planet signifying "death". It is the planet which 
signify pain. 
Rahu 
Rahu is a kin to Saturn but worse. In addition to the diseases 
signified by Saturn, diseases signified by Rahu are ulcer, 
hicough & inflammable gases. Rahu also causes diagnostic 
confusions. 
Ketu 
In addition to Rahu, Ketu also causes diagnostic confusions. 
It also indicates leproscopic & surgical interventions. It 
signifies leprosy & deformation of organs & all the diseases 
signified by Rahu.

CHAPTER - 7 
TRAVEL 
Travelling is undertaken by a native on account of education, 
service, business, medical treatment, marriage, pilgrimage, 
tourism etc. This travelling involves being away from home, 
city, state & country. 
Houses 
Houses involved in travelling are : 
3111 House: Away from home, away from motherland & 
short travels, 
gth House: Long journey, going to pilgrimage, foreign 
travel. 
12th House: Settling abroad, place away from motherland, 
long journeys. 
7th House: Changing the course of journey. 
Combinations, Timing, Conditions & Place of Visit 
If combination 3,9,12 is signified in DBA planets of separatives 
viz. Saturn, Rahu, Ketu & Sun, Native travels abroad. 
To pin point the travel further Astrologers should go ahead 
to use "Transits" .The general standard of the place visited 
is revealed by the planets of DBA If natural benefics Mercury, 
Jupiter, Venus, Sun, Moon are signified in DBA the native 
visits or settles in developed country. On the other hand if 
DBA planets are natural malefics Saturn, Rahu or Ketu he 
visits underdeveloped place I country. If DBA planets are 
both natural malefics & benefics developing place I nation 
is signified. 
- 94 -
Settling Abroad 
If the majority of the planets are signifying the combination 
of 3,9, 12 whereas 2,4, 11 is sinified in one or two planets 
the native settles abroad. 
Going in the Orbit, Space & to other Planets 
Neptune & Moon are the planets which signify Navigation. 
Moon of the native should be very strong. The native will go 
into orbit or to other "planets" if DBA planets ( Airy or in Airy 
Signs & separatives) will strongly signify 3,9,12 houses.DBA 
planets should also conjuct "Neptune" & "Uranus" signifying 
3,9,12 strongly.

The astrologers normally face a piquant situation when 
somebody asks as to when the native who had gone abroad 
& has not arrived at the scheduled time will come back 
home. 
There are various reasons for the native not coming back to 
his motherland at scheduled time like sickness, changing the 
course of the journey, extension of stay, death , litigation 
etc. 
a) Sickness : If the combination of 1, 6,8, is signified with 
3,9, 12 in the relevent DBA it is indicative that the native 
has fallen sick. When the combination of 5,11 is signified in 
the subsequent DBA the native is cured & will come back to 
the motherland in subsequent DBA indicating the combination 
2,4, 11. 
b) Changing the course of journey : If the 7th house is 
signified with the combination of 3,9, 12 the native has 
changed the course of the journey. 
c) Extension of Stay : If 3,9, 12 continues in the present 
DBA for quiet some time. 
d) Litigation : When combination of litigation appears with 
3,9, 12 person has extended his stay due to that reason. 
In all the above cases when the combination of 2,4,11 appears 
in the subsequent DBA the native comes back home. 
- 96 -
e) Death : When the longevity of the native is over native 
has died abroad. Refer to the chapter of "Longevity". 
General 
Majority of the planets of the natives connected with foreign 
services such as Ambassadors, Travel agents etc.have the 
combination of 3,9, 12. Ambassadors, Bureaucrates, Politicians 
travel to the foriegn countries in the DBA of Sun or Moon. 
Going to many countries at a time is signified by Mercury. In 
the DBA of Mercury native is likely to travel to a number of 
countries.

CHAPTER- 8 
CAREER & FINANCIAL 
PROSPECTS 
It is the desire of every person to have a successful career 
once he has taken plunge into it at a particular age & after 
finishing his studies. The pursuit of service or business as a 
career depends upon the family background, the education 
& other factors. Compelled by circumstances to earn a 
livelihood & support his family a person may have to start 
his career even without finishing his education. 
Astrologically whether a person will pursue service or business 
can be explained by the following combinations: 
For service 2, 6, 10, 11 
For business 2, 7, 10, 11 since these houses signify as 
under: 
Houses 
2"d House 
10th House

fame.

increments & promotion. 
It is the prime house for service & 
working conditions. 
It is prime house of business, business 
partners, trade & commerce. 
Therefore when 6th House connects 2,1 0,11 native joins a 
job whereas when 7th house is signified he joins business. 
Both 6th House & ih House are complementary houses hence 
practically it becomes difficult to understand whether the 
native will pursue business or job.

Promotions 
Native gets out of turn promotion & high ups when 
combination of 2,6,10,11 is signified in the DBA planets with 
favourable "Transits". A businessman in such a combination 
gets more profits. Regular promotion is sought even when 
2,6, 10,11 houses appear scattered in the DBA planets. 
Characteristics of Planets 
Sun/ Moon - Government 
Mars - Uniform 
Jupiter/Mercury/Venus - Corporate world. 
Rahu/ Ketu/Sat -Small establishments. 
A native is likely to ask from an astrologer is as to when will 
he get a job & whether in the Government or Private sector? 
This question is asked from the astrologer when the native 
is absolutely ready & willing to earn his livelihood. In the 
DBA of either Sun or Moon natives get a government job. In 
the DBA of other benefic planets native gets job in corporate 
world or public sector. In the periods of Rahu/ Ketu/Sat 
native gets job in small establishments. 
Interviews & Written Tests 
After the basic educational qualification there is a written 
test followed by "Group discussion" & Interviews. For 
success in the written test combinations of 6,11 or 10,11 
or4,9,11 or 5,11 or4,11 should be signified in DBA ( inthe 
descending order) planets during the written test . Transits 
of the planets should also be in the good Houses/ Nakshatra. 
For success in interview & group discussion the combinations 
3,6,11 or 3,10,11 or 3,11 should be signified by the DBA 
planets. The transits of favorable planets should also be in 
the favourable Houses I Nakshatra for the best results.

It is a common question which a native asks, whether he 
should do a job or business? If maximum number of planets 
signify good combination of profession then he should be 
advised to do business. On the contrary, if majority of planets 
signify bad combination of profession astrologer should advise 
him to do a job. 
Another question people ask as to what business should he 
do? It is a fact that if the Oasa periods running are good he 
is going to earn good whereas if periods running are bad he 
is going to suffer loss independent of the type of business 
he is doing. 
If Horoscope of native is bad then he will not be able to 
prosper in any type of business then he should be advised 
to offer his services rather than putting his own money in 
the business. Like if native is into jewellery business then 
he should not keep stock of jewellery. He should take the 
-112-
orders and get the jewellery manufactured or do the trading 
not involving his own money. Similarly if he is running a 
factory then he should first take orders and money for raw 
materials from the buyer and take only the labour charges. 
Another question which people ask is which name or first 
alphabet is suitable for the business? The answer is, name 
makes no difference to the business. The decisions taken 
by CMD in a public limited or private limited firm, the decisions 
taken by proprietor in proprietor firm depends upon the DBA 
of the CMD and proprietor respectively. If DBA signifies 
2,6, 10,11 decisions taken are favourable for the company 
on the contrary if 5,8, 12 is signified in the DBA, decisions 
taken are unfavourable for the company. In case of 
partnership firm the Horoscope of sleeping partners can not 
assert any influence on the success or failure of the firm. 
For more details readers are advised to read the chapter 
''Corporate Astrology" in the book. 
Role of Dasa, Bhukti and Antar (DBA) 
According to the principles of Nadi Astrology Dasa is stronger 
than Bhukti and Bhukti is stronger than Antar. Dasa in the 
life of Native signifies STATUS. Bhukti can give major losses 
and gains/ profits whereas Antar is not capable of giving 
any appreciable impact on the life of native unless or until 
supported by Bhukti. 
If good Dasa comes after bad Dasa or vice versa, there will 
be drastic change in the status of the native. If in good 
Dasa bad Bhukti arrives, bad Bhukti with bad Antar can give 
major loss but the status of the native will remain intact. 
The native can suffer major financial crunch in this period 
but the status will remain intact. If Dasa and Bhukti are 
good mere bad Antar will be ineffective to create any 
problem. Such an Antar can increase expenditure or native 
can purchase some assets.

Nature of a person 
Nature plays a major role in day to day activity and to do 
something major in life. Highly emotional, aggressive and 
depressive natives are not able to concentrate on work. 
Following are the type of nature people carry and 
astrological combination of the same: 
-118-
Aggressive nature 
Combination of 7,8, 12 signifies aggression. For nature since 
ascendant plays major role therefore any planet in 
Ascendant, Lord of Ascendant and any planet aspecting 
ascendant if it carries this combination, the native will be 
highly aggressive. More so if Pluto and Mars are in Ascendant 
signifying the combination native is a dacoit or terrorist. 
Fiery Ascendants Aries, Leo and Sagittarius are also 
aggressive but the lords of the Ascendants or planets in 
Ascendants and planets aspecting the Ascendant, if signify 
7,8, 12 then the native will be so aggressive that he will not 
be fit for social life. 
Depression 
Combination for depression is 2,1 ,6,8. Native will require lot 
of moral support to do anything significant in life. 
Emotional or sentimental 
Native born with Cancer as Ascendant are highly emotional. 
In decreasing order Native with Moon in Ascendant, Scorpio 
as ascendant and lastly Pisces Ascendant natives will also 
be emotional. However in all the cases lord of Ascendant or 
planet in Ascendant(Moon) should signify 6,8, 12 otherwise 
emotions will be largely in control. 
Cold Nature 
Houses 1 ,4,6, 10 give cold or indifferent nature. This nature 
is good for career as it does not carry emotions at all. 
Loosing balance of mind 
If depression and aggression combinations are together in 
a Horoscope, native will loose balance of mind. Illustration 
"CH3 C" shows the behaviour. Libra Ascendant natives are 
also prone to loose balance of their mind since the sign is 
"balance", airy and movable. Again care should be taken to 
asses this behaviour pattern. The lord of Ascendant or planet 
in the Ascendants should be signifying 6,8, 12 only then the 
native will loose the balance of mind otherwise not. 
Loving and Caring nature 
2,5,9, 11 Houses signify loving and caring nature. If majority 
-119-
of the planets signify this combination may not be good for 
career as native will be too generous and saintly as to be 
fit in material world. 
Character 
Combination of bad character is 5,8,12/ 7,8, 12. If maximum 
planets show the combination and are not supported by 
10,11 native will spend the money in vices. 
Bad Food habits 
Aggressive, Depressive and highly emotional natives have 
the tendencies to resort to bad food habits including 
excessive drinking, excessive smoking and even narcotics. 
Rahu, Ke~u or Saturn in the second House signifying 6,8, 12 
will promote these habits. This will obviously be negative 
signs for professional success and bad for health too. 
Spendthrift 
Native will be a spendthrift when Jupiter or Mars are in the 
Ascendant signifying 6,8, 12. 
Miser 
Native will be a miser if Saturn or Mercury are in the 
Ascendant signifying 6,8, 12.

Houses

Combination &. Timing of the Change in career 
When 5,9 houses are signified in the DBA of separative 
planets, person leaves the present job I business. Somehow 
if the DBA is not of the separatives native resigns during 
eventful "Transits" but the resignation is not accepted till 
DBA of separatives are there. In the subsequent DBA of 
separatives signifying the above combination the resignation 
is accepted & the native leaves the job. Readers must 
understand that any one of the DBA planet should be a 
-1 23-
separative planet viz. Sun, Rahu, Ketu or Saturn. Even if 
one of the DBA planets is aspected or conjunct with a 
separative planet the condition of change of job is fulfilled.

the exact period of change of job. 
Nature ofth~ Next Job/Business

in the career is with good prospects or not? If subsequent 
DBA signifies 2,6,10,11 or 2,7,10,11 then the change is for

with the promotion. However, if only 5,9 is signified in the 
DBA, the change is to the same status.

combination 5,9 , native has the tendency of changing the 
jobs frequently. Subsequent changes can be safely predicted 
by tracing subsequent DBAs & relevant transits to predict 
the same.

CHAPTER- 9 
MARRIAGE 
Marriage is association of two persons of opposite sex to 
live together & bring up a family. In India & other Asian 
countries today marriage is sacrosanct and is treated as 
fusion or coming together of two families of husband & wife 
respectively through this institution of marriage. 
However in the western countries it is an association of two 
persons to live together with or without getting married 
formally. 
Marriage being sacrosanct in Asian countries the customs, 
traditions the authorities & the courts have a tendency to 
discourage separation or divorce in spite of the fact that 
the two individuals do not have compatibility. 
Houses 
In astrology the Houses involved in marriage are: 
71h House: The prime house of marriage I partner I spouse. 
2"d House: The prime house of family. 
11th House: Gain of life partner I spouse I family. It also 
signifies fulfillment of desires. 
Combination & Timing of Marriage 
Marriage of native takes place in the DBA of the planets 
signifying the combination 2,7,11.To pin point the Marriage 
or the settlement of Marriage an Astrologer should check 
the Transits in the following way. First step of marriage is to 
finalize the boy and girl for marriage. 
- 141 -
The event of finalizing partner is natural event and after 
that engagement takes place and finally the marriage. Both 
engagement and marriage are social events and depend upon 
conveniences of relatives and religious warrants and rituals. 
Therefore it is rational to Time the event of finalizing the 
partner and after that we can go forward to check whether 
the marriage will take place or engagement will be broken. 
Transits 
Any of the three planets which signify the marriage may I 
may not be from the DBA should either 
• conjoin in one house 
• or aspect each other 
• or transit the significator houses 
• or aspect them 
• or transit over their natal positions 
After one of the above conditions is fulfilled the event can 
take place at any given moment. 
For example Jupiter, Mars and Sun signify marriage in any 
horoscope. In Transit Jupiter and Mars are conjunct and 
Sun joins both of them then this condition of conjunction of 
Mars, Sun and Jupiter all conjunct together will remain there 
for from a number of days to a month. We desire to calculate 
the day of the event. Hence after any of the above condition 
is fulfilled it is desirable to arrive at the day of the event. 
For that we proceed as follows. 
Event will take place on the day when 
A transiting planet signifying event, here it is marriage 
either transit exactly over Natal degree of planet 
signifying the event, marriage 
or two planets signifying marriage transiting in a sign 
conjunct with. in a degree. 
or any planet signifying marriage transit exactly over 
cuspal degree of 2, 7,11. 
- 142 -
or Moon conJOining three planets signifying event 
marriage. Note that Moon should also signify Marriage 
otherwise its Transit is not desirable to be seen. 
or the transit of the antar lord in the favourable 
Nakshatra. Favourable Nakshatra here also means 
Nakshatra of a Planet that signifies marriage or 2, 7, 11. 
Please note that event takes place when there is disturbance· 
in the cosmos within a degree or the sensitive degrees of 
Horoscope. Sensitive degrees of Horoscope are degrees of 
the Natal planets and the cuspal degrees of the Houses. In 
case of marriage, degrees of Planets signifying marriage and 
the cuspal degrees of 2,7, 11 Houses become important for 
pin pointing event. Also fast moving planets Sun, Mars, 
Mercury and Venus are be helpful in pin pointing the day of 
event since the others take a number of days to cover a 
degree whereas Moon is too fast to draw any conclusion. 
During Transits any Planet signifying the event can take 
part or it finally precipitates the event. We have normally 
three planets taking part in DBA and three planets in Transits 
involvements of which will finally make the event happen. It 
implies for a major event to happen in life minimum three 
planets are required. Fast moving planets finally play the 
role to time the date of event. In other words Transit of 
slow moving planets Saturn, Jupiter, Rahu or Ketu cannot 
finalize an event. 
Planet 
Venus is the natural significator of marriage for both the 
partners. Marriage of a native can take place without the 
involvement of Venus in the DBA But Venus is capable of 
promising marriage in its DBA even if it doesn't signify the 
combination of marriage i.e. 2, 7, 11 . 
Early and Late Marriage: This is a common question asked 
by parents or natives themselves. Early and Late are 
comparative words and also depend upon time, place and 
conditions. An Astrologer can see the earliest DBA signifying 
2,7,11 after marriageable age from where he can specify 
whether marriage will be early or late.

This is Horoscope of a male married in DBA of Mercury -
Mercury - Sun on 291h June 1979. To Time the period of 
Marriage we shall first consider the Time Place & Conditions. 
Keeping into view time place & conditions we will delineate 
this Horoscope. The Native becomes 24 years of age when 
the Bhukti of Jupiter starts in the Dasa of Saturn. For 
fructification/timing of an event DBA should allow the event, 
- 144-
here marriage. First Dasa lord should be analyzed if it signifies 
marriage then marriage can take place in the Dasa after 
that subsequent Bhukti should be analyzed further. If Dasa 
lord negates the marriage, Bhuktis will not give results. 
Astrologer should analyze the next Dasa in this case for 
marriage. Similarly if Dasa lord allows but Bhukti negates, 
next Bhukti should be analyzed for the fructification of event. 
After the agreement of the Bhukti lord, marriage will take 
place in Antar, which allows the event. After the Antar, 
Transits must endorse the event, which will finally give rise 
to fructification of marriage. 
Before analyzing marriage in the above Horoscope we must 
understand that there are three houses which take part in 
marriage and these are 2, 7, 11 . There are three houses which 
negate marriage and these are 1 ,6, 10. There are two houses 
5,9 which will always facilitate marriage. 5th being 1 ih from 
6th will negate no marriage, it is also house of love. gth House 
being 1 i" from 10th and House of general fortune. 
Two Houses 8 and 12 facilitate marriage when signified with 
2,7, 11 since 8th House signifies sexual parts and dowry. 1 ih 
House is house of bed pleasure. It also signifies expenditure 
in marriage. Please note that when the 1 ih House will be 
signified with 11th it means expenditure because the 
signification of 11th house means getting something in turn 
of spending. 1ih House will signify loss when signified with 
8th or 6th House. 
However these houses 8,12 will signify bickering, insults and 
loss to married life when they will be signified with 1,6,10 
houses. 
Houses 3, 4 remain neutral to marriage and the married life. 
When a native comes to an astrologer for query about 
marriage of native astrologer should see the DBA for marriage 
from the marriageable age. In this case at the marriageable 
age Dasa of Saturn and Bhukti of Jupiter was in operation. 
Dasa 
In this example Dasa lord Saturn allows marriage 
- 145 -
(see how to analyze a Planet for a particular event, here 
Saturn). The Planet, Nakshatra & Sub-lord of Saturn are as 
under: 
Sat 1 ,2,7 
Ven 3,5,10 
Rah 1,2,3,4,8,11,12 
The combination of marriage is 2, 7,11 & that of no marriage 
is 1 ,6,10. Houses, 5(Love); 8 (Dowry and sex); 12(bed 
pleasures); 9 (fortune) will act as supporting houses or 
facilitators or will support marriage Hoses 2, 7,11 . Houses 
3,4 will remain neutral. Now Saturn will be assessed in three 
capacities Planet, Nakshatra & Sub-lord. Sub-lord should be 
considered the strongest. First of all Planet should be 
analyzed. To analyze Planet, Nakshatra & Sub-lord we should 
check all the six Houses i.e. the three Houses which, signify 
marriage and three Houses which negate marriage & the 
Houses which facilitate marriage to get final picture. 
First the Planet: 
In this example Planet signifies two Houses, 2,7 of combination 
of marriage, 2, 7,11 & 1, one House of negation of combination 
Marriage i.e. 1 ,6, 10. Since the Houses of marriage are in 
majority the Planet signifies marriage. 
Then Nakshatra 
Nakshatra signifies none of the Houses of combination of 
Marriage but signifies House 10 of the combination of negation 
of marriage. It also signifies facilitator House 5. Therefore 
the Nakshatra negates marriage. 
Lastly Sub-Lord 
Sub-lord signifies two Houses; 2,11 of combination of marriage, 
2,7, 11 & 1, one House of negation of combination Marriage 
i.e. 1 ,6, 10. It also signifies Houses 8,12 i.e. the facilitator 
Houses. This implies that Sub-lord signifies marriage. 
Conclusion: 
To conclude, since Sub-lord strongly signifies marriage, 
although the Nakshatra is weak whereas Planet helps, 
marriage is possible in the Dasa of Saturn. However because

DOWRY 
Dowry is a tradition in India. Daughter receives her part of 
legacy in India at her marriage. Sometimes it becomes a 
matter of dispute and mishappenings to such an extent that 
brides are burnt alive. Some marriages are cancelled in certain 
cases and in some cases it results into bickering between 
the families of bride and groom and also result into divorces. 
The positive aspect of dowry is arrangement of things of 
basic and daily need for the new couple whereas negative 
aspects are bickering between the families and L'ndesired 
pain on petty issues. 
Combination of Dowry:Native receives dowry when 8,11 
is signified with the combination of marriage. 8
1
" House plays 
dual role in marriage. On one hand it is positive house for 
- 165 -
•marriage being the House of sexual parts and when it is 
signified with 11th House it also brings upon handsome dowry 
but on the other hand if it is signified with 12th House and/or 
6th House during marriage or immediately in following period 
it signifies bickering. When, House 8th House is signified with 
12tn House and/or 6th House, it amounts to ego, loss and 
aggressive nature. However, 8th House signified with 11th 
House and 12th House or 11th House and 6tn House will make 
things more complicated. It implies getting the dowry and 
denial and/or dispute after that on certain issues that will 
be discussed practically in illustrations to come. 
Getting Vehicle in Dowry: If House 4 along with 8,11 is 
signified and Venus is in DBA during marriage native gets 
vehicle in dowry. 
Combinations of getting the lstridhan back and loosing 
the Istridhan:During the trial of divorce between husband 
& wife many a times question of dowry arises which is con­
sidered legally as "Istridhan" or the belongingness of lady 
alone. If the combination of 2,6, 10,11 is signified in subse­
quent periods the lady gets the "istridhan" back. However if 
combination of 6,8, 12 appears in the subsequent period she 
looses it. 
Combination & Timing of Divorce 
The combination of 1,6,10 in the DBA of separative planets 
conveys disharmony & separations in the marital home. 
Further if 1 ,6, 10 is repeated in the subsequent DBA the 
couple gets a divorce from the court or the competent 
authority. For countries where divorce is considered as 
sacrosanct the involvement of 8,12 houses is essential since 
both the houses signify ego, hurdles, loss of all kinds, enemies 
etc. Also in these countries 1 ,6, 1 0,8, 12 for a long period is 
required because neither the society nor the law easily 
accepts the separation rather tries to reconcile the couple 
for a happy married life whereas in other countries 1 ,6, 10 
will signify divorce in DBA of Rahu, Ketu or Saturn. In 
cosmopolitan cities including India divorce is possible without 
the presence 8th and 12th House with 1 ,6, 10 which amounts 
to divorce with mutual consent.

Horoscope matching & married life 
Horoscope matching is done in order to warrant happy married 
- 171 -
life of the couple. 
Job of matching of horoscope starts after the general 
compatibility like the agreeable age difference, appearance, 
education, caste, creed and culture has been agreed upon. 
It is advised to the readers that matching of Horoscope is 
to be done before the finalization of the boy or the girl for 
marriage. There is no point coming to astrologer once the 
girl or the boy is finalized for marriage. 
If someone wants to get the horoscope of his daughter 
match with potentiai son in law then an astrologer 
should essentially check the following aspects of life: 
Longevity: First longevity should be checked carefully. If 
there is a perceptible difference between the longevity of 
the couple there is no point to match the horoscopes further. 
Reader can refer "Accurate Predictive Methodology" for more 
explanation on this aspect of life. 
Health: The boy in question should not have serious health 
problems. If otherwise the horoscope should be rejected 
altogether. 1 ,6,8, 12,4, 10 Houses promote bad health and 
no recovery from disease whereas 5,9, 11 Houses are best 
for good health. Reader can refer chapter "Health" for more 
explanation on this aspect of life. 
Nature: Following aspects should be considered for the 
nature of the boy 
Aggressive nature: For aggressive nature check 
number of 7,"8, 12 combinations in the planets. If 
combination 7,8,12 appears in 4 or more planets then 
the nature is aggressive. However if it appears similarly 
in girl's horoscope also then we can give a second 
thought to the Horoscope. For aggressive nature also 
see the Ascendant whether it is fiery and position of 
planets Mars and Pluto. If Mars and Pluto are in 1 st or 
3'd House signifying 7,8,12 houses, this is a position 
which describes that native is an extremist to the extent 
of becoming a dacoit or terrorist. Hence such a 
Horoscope should also be rejected. Fiery signs in 
Ascendant but the lords and the planets placed in the 
- 172-
Ascendant if do not signify 7,8, 12 should be positively 
taken. 
Depression: 2,1 ,6,8 is combination of depression. 
Sentiments: Cancer Ascendant when its Lord, Moon 
signifies 6,8, 12 Houses or Moon in Ascendant signifying 
Houses 6,8, 12 makes native sentimental. This cannot 
be matched with girl of aggressive nature. Whereas if 
Moon for Cancer Ascendant signifying other Houses is 
passable. 
Cohesive Nature: A number of 5,9, 1 i and also 2, 
?(with 2,5,9,11) give extremely loving and caring nature. 
Separative tendencies: Combination 1 ,6, 10 signifies 
that the native will not adjust with the wife and 1 ,4, 10 
gives dry nature. Nature will not be loving and caring. 
Ability to produce children: Combination of child birth are 
2,5,11. Boy having 1,4,10 suggests that in the periods of 
planets signifying 1 ,4, 10 native will not be able to produce 
children. This combination is also of impotency. 
Profession: It is desirable to see the Horoscope of the boy 
for profession. Houses 2,6,7,10,11 are positive for profession 
whereas Houses 5,8, 12 are negative for profession. Reader 
can refer "Accurate Predictive Methodology" for more 
explanation on this aspect of life. 
Character: If maximum planets in the Horoscope signify

However if 5,12 and 5,8 are signified then extramarital affair 
cannot surely happen in India and more so if 5,12 and 5,8

Sexual Compatibility:
