Manglik Dosh 
According to most of the Astrologers of present times if 
Mars occupies 1 ,2,4,7,8, 12 Houses from the Ascendant or 
Moon the native is a Manglik or having Manglik Dosh. Some 
Astrologers from South also believe that if Mars is in the 2nd 
House even then the Dosh exists and also the position of 
Mars should be seen from Venus also. No definite result of 
- 177 -
Manglik is known. Longevity and health are to be seen in 
individual Horoscope. Horoscope of any relative can not 
disturb Longevity. 
A Native is a Manglik, if Mars occupies 1 ,2,4,7,8, 12 Houses 
from the Ascendant, Moon or Venus i.e. if Mars is in 6 houses 
out of 12 houses then a Native is having Manglik Dosh. It 
implies that 50% of the population of the world must be 
Manglik. All positions are to be seen from Ascendant, Moon 
& Venus. This means we have to see the Dosh from three 
positions therefore there would hardly be a person "Non 
Manglik" on Earth. Even if a person has been declared a 
Manglik Astrologers are not clear what is going to happen. 
While delineating a Horoscope Astrologers consider one factor 
or the other, that too very broadly and give results. The 1 
advice is that they should analyze by all the Planets before 
coming to a conclusion. 
Conclusion 
Conclusion is that there is nothing called Manglik Dosh. 
Manglik has got more air from the time newspapers have 
started giving separate columns for Manglik in 
matrimonial columns. This is high time we should shed 
this myth, which has created a lot of chaos amongst 
people at large and is becoming instrumental in 
rejecting agreeable matches of boys and girls. 
GUN MILAN 
Literal meaning of Gun is the positive factors in personality 
of a native. It is desired to have a husband of good health, 
good profession for putting up the family, sexually active to 
the desire of wife, good nature, character and there should 
not be perceptible difference with the longevity of the wife. 
Similarly wife should also be having good health, sexually 
active to the desire of husband, good nature, and character 
and there should not be perceptible difference with the 
longevity of the husband. In most of the families in India 
wives are not working so the aspect of profession is 
conditional in this case. 
- 178 . 
Gun Milan as the name suggests should cover all the points 
raised in the compatibility of couple. But the Gun Milan of 36 
pts. is popular in which position of Moon in Signs and 
Nakshatras is considered of both boy and girl for matching 
the Horoscopes. All other planets are forgotten whose Dasa 
will also come in lifetime whereas Moon's Dasa may not come 
in life more so during married life. From the Gun Milan 
compatibility chart boy and girl should get more than 18 pts. 
then the Gun Milan is passable. Nadi Dosh is most dreaded 
of all, which explains that if boy and girl's Nakshatra are the 
same, Adya, Madhya or Antya there is Nadi Dosh which is 
such a dreaded word as if heavens will fall. 
Conclusion 
It is desired to have spouse of good health, good 
profession, sexually compatible, good nature and 
character and there should not be perceptible difference 
between the longevity ofthe two. In other words these 
are the "Gun" of a native. Gun Milan is something other 
than this therefore should not be considered at all during 
checking compatibility of the life partners.

CHAPTER -10 
CHILDREN 
A person has a social obligation to bring up children after 
marriage. In India it is gratifying for the parents when they 
are blessed with children more particularly a son, as the 
birth of the son leads to a continuity of the cast or clan to 
which the father belongs. Child birth therefore is a gain of a 
member in the family of the native. 
Houses 
Astrologically houses involved in the birth of the child are : 
2"d House: It signifies family. 
51h House: It is the prime house of children. 
11th House: It signifies gain of children & fulfillment of 
desires. 
gth House: It doesn't play any significant role in child birth 
directly but promotes child birth. It is facilitator house for 
child birth. 
Planet 
Jupiter: It is natural significator for child birth. The mere 
presence of Jupiter in DBA results into child birth even if it 
doesn't signify the combination of child birth. 
Combinations & the birth of Child 
Birth of the child takes place in the DBA of planets signifying 
houses 2,5,11. For child birth the time of conception is to 
be given more consideration than the actual time of delivery 
of the child. In addition to this the female horoscope should 
be given more consideration than the male horoscope 
because male's role is restricted to giving a seed whereas 
- 185 -
female receives the seed, carries the foetus in the womb 
for nine months & then gives birth to the child if everything 
goes well during the nine months of pregnancy. 
No Child 
Combination of 1,4,10 indicates the native is unlikely to 
have children. DBA of planets signifying the combination will 
rule out the possibility of child birth. 
Houses 
1st House : 1st house is 12th from the 2nd house signifying 
family. 
41h House : 4th house is 12th from the 5th house signifying 
children. 
1 01h House : 1Oth house is 12th from the 11th signifying 
gain of child. 
Note : If the Nakshatra &/ or the Sub lord of Jupiter signify 
the combination 1 ,4, 1 0 clearly it will also render the planet 
weak to promote child birth. 
Number of Children 
The number of children a native will have depends upon 
number of times 2,5, 11 appears in all the Planets in the 
Horoscope of a Native clearly. 
Pregnancy Periods & Miscarriages 
During the period of pregnancy if the combination of 1 ,4, 10 
along with separatives Saturn, Rahu, Ketu in DBA is in 
operation it causes miscarriage. In transit the separatives 
planets either transit or aspect the 1 ,4, 10 houses or transit 
over their natal position. Their transits over the 2,5, 11 should 
also be considered bad. However if the DBA of natural 
benefics is in operation signifying combination of 2,5,11 both 
mother & the chird in the womb enjoy good health. The 
development of child will be satisfactory. DBA of benefics 
after the 6th month to the delivery of the child results into 
good weight of the child at delivery & on the contrary bad 
houses 1 ,4, 1 0,6,8, 12 scattered in the DBA of natural malefics 
- 186 -
from the 6th month to the delivery period results into weak 
child or premature birth of the child. 
Abortion 
Normally an abortion is resorted to when there is danger to 
the life of the mother or the child or the scanning of the 
womb shows birth of a deformed child or on humanitarian 
grounds of rape or otherwise after the sex of the child is 
known or the child is not wanted. In all such cases DBA will 
clearly indicate the combination of 5,8, 12 along with the 
malefics Saturn, Ketu & Rahu . During the abortion these 
planets either transit or aspect the 5th house. 
Special Cases 
If in a horoscope 5th house of native has presence of natural 
malefics signifying Houses 1 ,4, 1 0,6,8, 12 in Nakshatra & 
Sub lord, it implies number of mishaps during pregnancy 
periods. Similarly the transit of these planets over the 5th 
house is also very bad. 
Still Birth 
Another distinguishing feature of child birth is when the 
dasa of Saturn is in operation at the time of delivery which 
results into still birth of the child. Of course Saturn should 
signify bad houses in the case too. 
Gand Mool Nakshatras 
It is a belief that child born in "Gand Mool Nakshatra" is 
likely to face series of obstacles & mishaps in his life time & 
the birth is considered to be inauspicious to family members. 
By Gand Mool Nakshatra we mean the native taking birth 
in the Nakshatras of Mercury or Ketu. 
If the Dasa of Mercury or that of Ketu does not signify 
6,8,12 at the time of birth, child will not face any problem. 
However if they signify 6,8,12 various problems are faced 
by the child. This holds true not only in the Dasa of Gand 
Mool Nakshatras ( Mercury or Ketu ) but also in the Dasa of 
other planets. 
- 187 -
There is a common question asked to the astrologers whether 
the child born is auspicious for parents & other relatives or 
not ? The astrologer in such a case should explain to the 
parents that the child has come into existence & not the 
parents have come into existence from the child. 
Sex of the Child 
Sex of the child can safely be predicted by an astrologer 
with a simple calculation however the professional ethics 
do not warrant that the astrologer should predict the sex 
of the child as it is likely to create chaotic social conditions. 
Adoption 
The childless couples normally resort to adoption of the 
child once they are advise·d that they are not likely to have 
any child. In the Horoscope of child the combinations of 
4,8 in DBA of dual planets Mercury or Jupiter will signify the 
time of adoption of the child. Dual sign should also fall in 
the 8th house or Mercury or Jupiter should be in the 8th 
house in the Natal Horoscope to signify dual legacy. 
Custody 
Coming back to the litigation in marriage during the trial of 
divorce between husband & wife the vital question between 
husband & wife & the courts & other authorities is which of 
the parents should be entrusted with the custody of the 
children. 
If combination of 3,9 is signified in the DBA planets at the 
particular time in the horoscope of the child, father gets 
the custody of the child or in the other words child goes to 
the father since 3'd House is 1 ih from mother and gtn House 
is that of the father. However if combination of 4,8 is 
signified in the DBA of the child the child goes to the mother 
since 4th House is the House of mother whereas 8th House is 
1 ih from the House of father. 
But if the combination of 3,8 is in the DBA of the child the 
custody is entrusted neither to father nor mother but other 
relatives or persons. Same combination appears when the 
child is exchanged in the hospital.

CHAPTER 11 
CORPORATE 
ASTROLOGY 
Head hunting for a body corporate has become an "in thing" 
in the leading corporate circles of the world. All big corporate 
& multinationals have started taking the help of astrology to 
pick up the right person for the right job. 
Any entity created by law of the land for carrying out any 
commercial activity is called a body corporate. Others include 
sole proprietorship concerns, partnership firms & companies. 
Sole proprietorship concerns & partnership firms are ruled by 
one or few individuals & body corporate is ruled by many 
people including participation from public. 
A corporate entity means & implies an association, cooperative 
or a group of persons with a large capital base & large 
commercial activity. 
From astrological point of view since corporate are ruled by 
various individuals including the chief executive officer the 
horoscopes of key persons become relevant for its running, 
operations & other prospects. Since corporate body is run 
by various individuals a few persons coming & going does 
not make a special dent on the body corporate that is why 
it is said in corporate circles that individuals come and go 
but a corporate body lives. Therefore a body corporate is 
equated & treated like a deemed person. 
From our point of view it becomes relevant that the key 
persons have the appropriate combinations in relation to 
the business carried out by the body corporate. Similarly 
the next step also should have the right combination for 
various departmental heads e.g. the chief executive officer 
of the body corporate engaged in building & construction 
- 196 -
should have combination of 4,11 in most of the planets. In 
the next step any departmental head is working in the finance 
& accounts of the same company should have combination 
of 2,3, 10,11 & good Mercury & good Jupiter. Similarly quality 
control departmental head should have good Mercury, Venus 
& combination of3,5,10,11. 
If construction company is engaged in quality housing Venus 
must be good & alternatively if the company is engaged in 
housing of masses Mars & Saturn should be preferably good 
of that of the relevant officers. 
In the cases if the horoscope of the individuals are not 
available "Prasna Charts" are relevant which always give 
accurate results. 
This way astrology plays a very significant role in growth 
and prospects of a corporate. If these combination are 
considered at the time of appointment of the Chief Executive 
Officers & the key officers of various departments. 
Since a body corporate is deemed to be an individual a natal 
horoscope can be drawn by taking date, time & place of 
birth of the representatives of the corporate to the 
registering authority but however the horoscope of the Chief 
Executive Officer & other departmental heads are more 
important than the natal horoscope of the body corporate. 
I have discussed the case of body corporate if it is engaged 
in the construction work. Similarly for a manufacturing body 
engaged in assembly unit should have good Jupiter & 
combination of 2,6, 10,11 or a body corporate having trading 
unit should have good Mercury & combination of 2,7, 10, 11. 
Similarly: 
For Manufacturing of Vehicles 4,11 &. Venus 
Manufacturing Soda Ash or Cement 2,6,10,11 &Saturn 
Mining & Minerals 8,11&Saturn 
Textiles units 2,6, 10,11 & Moon 
Export units 2,6,10,11 & 3,9,12 etc.

CHAPTER -12 
LONGEVITY 
Longevity is the time a person lives on this planet, Earth. 
Death according to astrology occurs when the native takes 
his last breath. 
Houses 
Houses which increase the life of a native are : 
1,5,9, 10 & 11 
Death inflicting Houses are : 
6, 8, 12, Badhak & Marak Houses. 
Badhak Houses 
Badhak Houses are as follows : 
1) If the Ascendant is movable i.e. any of the 1 ,4,7, 10 
sign is in the ascendent then the badhak house is the 
11th House. 
2) If the Ascendant is fixed i.e. any of the 2,5,8, 11 
sign is in the ascendent then the badhak house is the 
9th House. 
3) However, if the Ascendant is common i.e. any of the 
3,6,9,12 sign is in the Ascendant then the badhak house 
is the 7th House. 
Badhak Houses as they are classified by their name appear 
to give obstacles in the life time of the native. On the 
contrary they act as death inflicting planets at the time of 
death only. 
Marak Houses 
Marak houses are the 2nd & 7th houses in all horoscopes. 
They act as death inflicting houses at the time of death 
only. 
- 202 -
4th House 
4th house in addition to other significances also signify "end 
of everything". Hence it is relevant to consider this house 
here. At the time of death this house also promotes death & 
transits of the planets in this house are very significant. 
Planet 
Saturn is the natural significator of death. Saturn plays a 
major role at the time of death.It is either DBA lord or is 
conjunct with any of the DBA lord. In transits also it plays 
major role. It transits in the 6,8,12 or Marak or Badhak houses 
of the native at the time of death. Pluto also plays similar 
role more profoundly in unnatural deaths. 
After the DBA has signified death, Transits of planets will 
exactly pin point death. At the time of death Moon transits 
in the 4,6,8, 12 or Marak or Badhak houses/ Nakshatras. 
Calculation of Longevity 
Longevity of a native can be calculated by adding the sum 
total of life increasing houses (1,5,9,10,11)ofseven planets 
excluding Rahu & Ketu with their Nakshatra & Sub Lords. For 
Rahu & Ketu only the planet has to be considered. Similarly 
the sum total of death inflicting houses ( 6,8,12,badhak) is 
to be arrived in the seven planets & Rahu & Ketu. Formula of 
the Longevity is as follows : 
,A. 
Longevity (in years) = 'A"7R X 120 (of Vimshottari Dasa) 
where A is sum total of life increasing houses as calculated 
above. & B is the sum total of death inflicting houses as 
calculated above. The exception to the rule of longevity is 
the house of badhak. The badhak houses are to be added to 
death inflicting houses. The Marak houses do not come into 
play in the calculation of longevity in the formula. 
In addition to the above formula cuspal sub lord of the 
Ascendant should be carefully analysed. If the first house 
Cusp signifies death inflicting houses only the life is small ( 
- 203 -
Alpayu) . In such a case there is no use of calculating the 
Longevity by the formula given above. If The Strong death 
inflicting planets & bad transis along with Saturn coming in 
the DBA will result into death of the Native. However if 
cuspal sub lord of the Ascendant signifies life increasing 
houses only then the native has long life say above 85years. 
If the cuspal sub lord of the Ascendant signifies both life 
increasing houses and death inflicting houses then the 
longevity is medium. 
Combinations & Timing the Death 
Death of the native takes place in the DBA of planets 
signifying houses 6,8,12, Marak or Badhak in the planet, 
Nakshatra & Sub lord. After calculating the years of Longevity 
by the above formula we should analyse the strongest of 
the death inflicting planets in the DBA near to the years of 
longevity as arrived. This DBA will result into death of a 
native. Involvement of Saturn in DBA should be there as 
discussed above. Its transit should also be carefully analysed 
with the transits of the other death inflicting planets in the 
natal horoscope to pin point Longevity. 
To conclude the chapter of Longevity, it is advisable to all 
not to predict Longevity of any native under any circum­
stances. 
Readers must be curious to know why & when the death of 
people take place in following circumstances. 
Death at the time of Marriage 
The combination of marriage as discussed in the chapter of 
marriage is 2, 7,11 . If the Ascendant is movable 11th house 
becomes the Badhak. Therefore if the longevity calculated 
according to the formula given is up to the marriage of the 
native the native dies at the time of marriage. 
Accidental Death 
The combination for accident & injury is 1, 4, 6, 8,12 & 
death inflicting Houses are 6,8, 12.The longevity of the native 
can be checked at the time of the accident by an astrologer 
- 204 -
by the same formula. If the longevity of the native finishes 
at the time of accident native dies in the accident. 
Death in Foreign Land 
Death in foreign land can also be checked by the formula & 
the death inflicting DBA with the combination of 3,9, 12 will 
signify death abroad. 
Death after disease or during Surgery 
For all cases of chronic disease or disability Saturn's 
involvement is there with the combination of 6,8, 12. So 
death of a person during surgical operations, sickness can 
also be explained by the fact that Saturn & 6,8, 12 are death 
inflicting planets.

CHAPTER -13 
RECTIFICATION OF 
BIRTH TIME 
Prediction in "Natal Astrology" depends upon the Horoscope. 
The Horoscope of a Native is cast on the basis of the Date, 
Time, Place of birth provided to the Astrologer. Date & Place 
of birth given to the Astrologer is normally correct. Although 
there is correction required in the Time due to number of 
reasons. One, there used to be Mechanical watches few 
years ago & these watches used to loose or gain time from 
minutes to hours in a day. Moreover in the villages there 
were no watches at all. Therefore it is always advisable to 
verify these Horoscopes. Secondly, there is always a 
confusion in the mind of Astrologers as to what should be 
the birth time : 
1) When the child comes out of the "Womb". 
2) When the "Cord" is cut. 
3) When the child breathes for the first time or in the 
other words child's "Cry" is heard for the first time 
after coming out of the "Womb". 
L.asUy, sometimes it becomes a secondary issue to note the 
time of birth in case of complications during the delivery 
time. 
Therefore before making the predictions an able Astrologer 
should check whether the Horoscope cast of the "Native" is 
correct or not. First & the foremost step to check whether 
the Horoscope is correct or not is to tally the events that 
have happened in the life of the native already by the relevant 
DBA & Transits. If they tally, the Horoscope is correct on 
the other hand if the events do not tally the horoscope 
requires "Birth Time Rectification". By simple application of 
- 207 -
mind an astrologer can know whether the time difference is 
little or not. But if the Horoscope looks to be totally vague 
or the Native himself is keen to know his "Ascendant" since 
he is not sure about his birth time the theory of ruling planets 
should be applied for "Birth Time Rectification" for accurate 
results. 
Care should be taken while applying the theory of "Ruling 
Planets". The theory should be applied when the native is 
keen to know about his Ascendant. The theory can also be 
applied if the relative of any native is keen to know about 
the Native's Horoscope. The theory is also valid when the 
Astrologer is inclined to correct the Horoscope of the Native. 
Ruling Planets 
There are four ruling planets to be considered at the time of 
birth rectification 
1) Day Lord: Day lord is the lord of the day i.e. on Sunday 
the day lord shall be Sun, on Monday day lord shall be 
Moon & so on. 
2) Moon Sign lord: It is the lord of the sign in which 
Moon is transiting on the day. 
3) Nakshatra lord: It is the lord of the Nakshatra in which 
Moon is transiting on the day. 
4) Ascendant lord: It is the lord of the Ascendant rising 
at the time when the query is put to the astrologer. 
Step 1 
To take out the degree of the Ascendant astrologer should 
note down the time at which the question (What is my 
Ascendant?) is asked by the Native in person or on phone or 
by Fax. If the question is asked by the native on "Internet" 
or by a simple letter astrologer should note down the time 
at which the question is received by the astrologer. The 
place is to be taken where the Astrologer is present at the 
time of receiving the question. In case astrologer is interested 
himself about knowing the degree of the Ascendant of a 
Horoscope he should note down the time at which he has 
- 208 -
actually started working upon the Horoscope. 
Step2 
Take out the ruling planets at the time the query is made. 
Step 3 
Now take up the Horoscope made from the approximate 
time given by the Native. 
Step4 
Take the Table of Sign Lord, Nakshatra lord & Sub lord from 
the Annexure. 
StepS 
Now we have to proceed to arrive at the Ascendant, 
Nakshatra & the Cuspal Sub lord from the Ruling planets 
arrived on from the "Step 2". 
For example ruling planets are Jupiter, Mercury & Moon. The 
Ascendant of the Horoscope which is made by the 
approximate time given by the Native is "Capricorn". Saturn 
is the lord of the Ascendant, Capricorn but it is not one of 
the ruling planets. Therefore the conclusion is that Capricorn 
is not the Ascendant. The nearest Ascendants to Capricorn 
are "Sagittarius" & "Aquarius". For instance we take up 
Ascendent to be Aquarius. Aquarius can not be the Ascendant 
too since the lord of Aquarius is Saturn again. Now we go on 
to investigate Sagittarius which can be the Ascendant since 
Jupiter is one of the Ruling planet. In the Sign Sagittarius 
there are three Nakshatras & those are of Ketu, Venus and 
Sun. All these Nakshatra lords are notfrom the Ruling planets 
therefore we will rule out this Ascendant too. Similarly 
Scorpio & Libra can not be the Ascendents. Now let us find 
out whether "Pisces" can be the Ascendant or not. Pisces is 
the Sign ruled by Jupiter which is one from the Ruling planets. 
Pisces sign has three Nakshatras & those are Jupiter, Saturn 
& Mercury. Mercury can be the Nakshatra where the degree 
of the Ascendant may be falling because Mercury is one of 
the Ruling planet. The exact degree of the Ascendant will 
- 209 -
fall in the Sub lord of Moon in the Nakshatra of Mercury & 
the Sign of Jupiter because all the Sub lord, Nakshatra, Sign 
lord are from the Ruling planets. The Ascendant degree in 
the Sign of Pisces, Nakshatra of Mercury & Sub of Moon is 
from 22:13:20 to 23:20 . 
Step 6 
Now the Horoscope is cast by taking down the degree of 
the Ascendant by the "Step 5". Since we had arrived at 
"Capricorn" as Ascendant by the time given by the Native 
we'll have to increase the time proportionately to arrive at 
the present degree of the Ascendant, Pisces. 
Step7 
After casting the horoscope of the Pisces Ascendant we will 
investigate the major events of the life of the Native by the 
methods given in this book. By looking on to the events in 
the DBA of the Native we'll now correct the degree of the 
Ascendant more precisely. 
Now I'll like to share a practical example of the "Birth Time 
Rectification" with the readers.

CHAPTER -14 
HELPING SCIENCES 
There are many occult sciences viz. Palmistry, Numerology, 
Face reading etc. Out of all of the occult sciences "Astrology" 
is the most accurate. However other sciences can be taken 
as helping sciences to Astrology. 
Numerology 
Numerology is a science of numbers from 1 to 9. In 
numerology, numbers 1-9 are classified into three groups : 
a) 1,2,4,7 
b) 4,5,8 
c) 3,6,9 
According to numerology important events in a native's life 
take place during the multiples of the set of numbers in 
which the native was born. 
Important events of the natives taking birth on 1 ,2,4,7 or 
the numbers aggregating to the similar dates of any month 
i.e. 1,2,4,7, 11,13,16,19, 20,22,25,28,29,31 dates of a month 
will take place on the same dates & multiples of similar years 
i.e. 1 ,2,4,7. 
Similarly important events of the natives taking birth on 
4,5,8 dates or numbers aggregating to the similar dates of a 
month will take place on the same dates & multiples of similar 
years i.e. 4,5,8. 
In the same way important events of the natives taking 
birth on 3,6,9 dates or numbers aggregating to the dates of 
a month will take place on the same dates & multiples of 
similar years i.e. 3,6,9. 
- 214 -
In Numerology master numerologist is considered to be 
"Cheiro". According to him the number 8 is of vital importance 
& relevance. According to him number 8 is number of 
extremes. Astrology also has similar views House number 8 
is house of extremes. Persons taking birth on 8,17,26 date 
of a month either reach very high or low in career, name & 
fame etc. Number 8 is ruled by planet Saturn. It has been 
observed that people taking birth on these dates have to 
face various troubles in life. The life is full of miseries & 
misfortunes in various spheres. The problems are very difficult 
to overcome. On the other hand for few persons the number 
is extremely fortunate but they are very less in percentage. 
The number is so significant that it is found that people 
living in house no.8 have multiple or a single problem. Either 
there will be sickness in the family or the family is blessed 
with the daughters only or there will be penury in the family 
etc. In a few extreme cases the persons will be extremely 
rich & fortunate. Therefore bed no.8 or room no.8 in hospitals 
or nursing homes should be avoided. This number should 
also be avoided in all the spheres of life like bank account, 
vehicle registration number aggregating to no.8 e.g. 6101 ; 
6+1+0+1 =8. 
Similarly No.5 is number of love . People born on the dates 
5,14,23 of any month have multiple love affairs. They like 
association with people of opposite sex & are fascinated by 
luxuries. They indulge in speculative gains. 
People born on 4,22,13 dates of any month are eccentric in 
behaviour. Number 4 also rules emotions. Number 9 is 
considered to be most fortunate number. People taking birth 
on 9,18,27 of any month are most fortunate. 
Important 
People born on nearly same time have different patterns of 
life . Therefore it is impossible to predict accurately on the 
basis of date of birth. Moreover if you divide the total 
population of the world, which is appox. 600 crores by 9 we 
arrive at a figure of 65 crores appox. therefore reader has 
to make his own judgment about the accuracy of this occult 
science. 
- 215 -
Palmistry 
This science can give an idea for various events of life. 
Knowledge about these occult sciences & others helps an 
astrologer in many arenas. 
Important 
Palmistry also has its own limitations one, it can not predict 
beyond the palm & secondly palmistry is not as accurate as 
Astrology. Furthermore lines of hands keep on changing 
throughout the life.

CHAPTER-15 
MUHURAT 
Muhurat as commonly understood means the most auspicious 
time for commencing any important event. Before I deal 
with the Muhurat I would like to describe a natal horoscope. 
Natal horoscope is a tabular chart of the position of planets 
in the sky at the date, time & place of birth of the native. 
This natal horoscope gives a wide spectrum of various events 
which are going to happen during the life time of a native. 
Coming back to Muhurat we have to draw a similar tabular 
chart for commencement of any single event of life of the 
native which has been referred to the astrologer for Muhurat. 
Therefore we draw a horoscope of the date, time & place of 
birth when the event is likely to give the best results for 
that event. It means we have to pick up the specific 
combination in DBA of the particular event which have already 
been refered in this book & recommend the auspicious time 
for the referred Muhurat. The astrologer should also bear in 
mind the single most step to be taken for that event for 
commencement of the activity for which the Muhurat have 
been referred to e.g. if the Muhurat has been referred to 
commencement of construction of property the first step 
should be digging of earth by the accepted tool of 
construction. 
According to me Muhurat is the best planetary position at 
the given time & nothing else is to be considered for the 
same however "pooja" being a formality can be preformed at 
any time so desired by the native. 
One word of caution for the astrologer to be borne in mind 
& that is the muhurat can not nullify or dilute the effects of 
natal horoscope to a large extent but it certainly can be 
used as a remedial measure for reducing the ill effects if 
proper muhurat is adopted by the native with the help of 
- 217 -
able astrologer. 
Now I will deal with the various combinations for different 
events which should assist the astrologer in finding out the 
muhurat of a particular event. 
For Purchase & Construction of Property: In both the 
cases DBA planets should clearly signify 4,11 in the 
Nakshatras & Sub lords. The Nakshatras & the Sub lords of 
the DBA so derived should not signify 6,8,12. It is preferable 
in the case of construction of property that the DBA should 
continue to signify 4,11 till the desired time of construction. 
Houses other than 4,11 and 6,8, 12 should be considered as 
neutral. 
Grah Parvesh: After purchasing I constructing a property 
the next step is to live happily with the family. The first step 
to enter in the house will mark the muhurat of entering the 
new house after completion of the same. In such a case 
DBA planets should clearly signify 2, 5, 11 & 9 in their 
Nakshatras & Sub lords. Majority of the planets of the 
"Muhurat horoscope" should signify the same combination. 
Purchase of a Vehicle: DBA of natural benefic at the time 
of taking out vehicle from the show room should signify 
3,4,5,11 in the planets, Nakshatras & Sub lords, preferably 
for a quite a number of years. However subsequent DBA can 
signify 3,5, 10 which only signifies the sale of the same. 
Combination of 6,8, 12 should not be present in the DBA 
Purchase a Machine: Same as that of the purchase of 
vehicle. 
Employment: Native should join a job when the DBA of 
natural benefics planets signify 2,6, 10,11 in the Nakshatra 
& Sub lord. However planet may signify 5,9. Nakshatra or 
Sub lord should not signify 6,8, 12 houses. 
Starting of Business: At the time of first transaction 
2,7,10,11 should be signified by the DBA of natural benefics 
in the Nakshatras & Sub lords. 
Travel: At the time when the native keeps first step out of 
- 218 -
the house for the journey or starts the vehicle for travel or 
journey DBA of natural benefic planets should signify 5,9,11 
in the Nakshatras & the Sub lords. In this case too Houses 
6, 8, 12 should be avoided. 
Marriage: Houses 2, 7,11, 5, 9 should be signified in the 
DBA planets' Nakshatras & Sub lords when the first step 
towards marriage is taken like wearing ring. 1 ,6, 1 0,8, 12 should 
not be signified in the Nakshatras & the Sub lords of DBA 
planets. 
Partnership: Partnership deed should be signed when the 
DBA planets, Nakshatras & Sub lords signify the houses 
3,5,11.Combination 6, 8, 12 should not be signified in the 
DBA planets, Nakshatras and Sub lords. 
Filing a Legal Case: DBA planets, Nakshatras and Sub lords 
should signify the houses 6,11 or 10,11 at the time of filing 
the case. Houses 6, 8, 12 should be avoided here too since 
they signify loosing the legal case. 
Education: While taking out the muhurat of starting 
education care should be taken that Natural malefics should 
not be in the 4th house, Combinations of 3,6,8 or 3,8, 12 or 
6,8, 12 should not be present in the Nakshatras & Sub lords 
of the DBA planets & in the subsequent DBAs also. Houses 
4,5,9, 11 ,2,3 should be present in the DBA of Natural benefics 
planets' Nakshatra & Sub lord & in the subsequent DBA's. 
Interview: Time for appearing in the interview is not in the 
hands of the natives. So the native should set out for the 
interview when the relevent DBA of Natural benefics preferably 
signifies the combination of 3,6, 11 in the Nakshatras & Sub 
lords. 
Sowing Seeds: The present & the subsequent DBAs of 
natural benefics more preferably Jupiter, should signify houses 
2, 5, 9, 11 in the Nakshatras & the Sub lords. In this case 
1 ,4, 10 and 6, 8, 12 should be avoided. 
Starting a treatment: Some diseases like TB require a long 
treatment. If possible treatment of such a disease should 
start when the DBA of benefics (preferably Jupiter) signify 
- 219 -
the combination of 5,11 & other good houses 1, 9 in the 
Nakshatras & Sub lords. Subsequent DBAs should also be 
good to give a catalytic effect to the whole treatment. 
Surgical Operation: At the time of surgery DBA of natural 
benefic planets should signify combination of 1, 5, 9, 11 . It 
is advisable to avoid 4, 1 0, 6, 8, 12. 
Speculation: 3rd , 5th & 8th houses should be signified by 
planets & 6,1 0,11 houses by Nakshatras & Sub lords of DBA 
planets signifies success in speculation of all sorts. In case 
of gambling native should join the party for gambling or start 
playing in the Casinos etc. in the similar combination discussed 
above. 
Playing Match: Matches start at times set by the organizers 
therefore native should either enter the field or set out for 
the match from home at the time when DBA planets, 
Nakshatras & Sub lords signify the combination of 6,1 0, 11. 
Films I Plays: Screening or making a film or staging of plays 
should be done when DBA planets signify 3,5,8 in planets & 
their Nakshatras & Sub lords signify 6,10,11. The DBA should 
be preferably of Venus and Mercury or Venus or Mercury. 
Applying for Visa: It is difficult to get Visa of developed 
countries by the natives of developing & underdeveloped 
countries. While applying for Visa DBA of natural benefic 
planets should signify 3,9,12 in the planets & 3, 6, 10, 11 in 
the Nakshatra & Sub lord. 
Writing I Publishing a Book: DBA of natural benefic planets 
most preferably Mercury should signify 3,11. 
Advertisement: Same as that of writing I publishing a book. 
Religious rituals: Nakshatras & Sub lords of the natural 
benefics DBA planets should signify 5,9, 10, 11. 
Starting a War: Nakshatras & Sub lords of the DBA planets 
& subsequent DBA planets should clearly signify 6,1 0, 11. 
Digging a Well: Nakshatras & Sub lords of the DBA of Natural 
- 220 -
benefic planets should signify the combination of 8, 11 in 
the Nakshatra & Sub lord.

CHAPTER16 
REMEDIAL MEASURES 
By now the readers of this book might have realised that all 
the events whether good or bad are regulated by the position, 
aspect, conjunction of planets, signs & houses. It strongly 
implies that these events are predestined and the various 
combinations given in this book support the principle that 
"Destiny can not be changed". 
However, the impact of good or bad dasa can be enhanced 
or reduced through the remedial measures adopted by the 
native as per the recommendation of an able astrologer only 
in exceptional cases where mixed trends are indicated. 
Remedial measures as known in traditional astrology are 
Yantra, Mantra, Tantra, Donations, Precious stones etc. 
Pooja of deity: Pooja of deity implies to learn from the life 
and message of deity. It does not imply to perform the 
rituals only. Image of Lord Ganesh gives us a message how 
an ideal administrator should be. An administrator can also 
learn from the image of Lord Ganesh. Elephant head implies 
that an administrator must have brain and memory like an 
elephant. Big ears indicate that an administrator should listen 
exhaustively to all before reaching to any conclusion. He 
should smell everything going on with his long trunk. His 
eyes should not be expressive so that no one could 
understand what is going on in his mind. He should have big 
tummy to digest what he has listened from people and 
informers and keep restrain from talking uselessly. However 
when he should speak his message should be clear and loud 
like an elephant trumpet. Book in hand of Lord Ganesh 
indicates that he should be learned. Mouse ride indicates 
that an administrator should manage his life in minimum 
means. And that is how you will get "Ladoo" in the life 
which is on the other hand of Lord Ganesha. These learnings 
- 223 -
will lead a native to success in life, so called pooja is a mere 
formality. 
Pooja: Parmhans Yoganand explains in his book 
"Autobiography of a yogi", "A child is born on the day and 
that hour when the celestial rays are in mathematical 
harmony with individual Karma". It implies that what you 
sow that you reap. Pooja can not.undo wrongs committed. 
While pooja one can pledge in front of almighty that he will 
keep his positive approach towards life and that is what will 
help in making his life more easy more simple and more 
purposeful. 
Donation: Let us understand what a donation is. Donation 
is an offering to a needy whereas donor does not expect 
anything in return or it is "Niswarth sewa" .The donation 
can be given by any of the means possible "Tan, Man or 
Dhan". Offering to planets or giving articles as advised by 
"Astrologers" does not fit into the definition of donation. It 
is done with Swarth. Now the question is what will be the 
effect of the offerings. The obvious answer is a big "Zero". 
However donation is top most remedy since this is your 
positive "Karma" and the effect expected are better in return. 
One thing I will still like to point out is that although it is 
best remedy no one can find out when its positive effects 
will come into pass. 
Vaastu: Vaastu is Hindi/ Sanskrit synonym to architecture. 
Let us discuss simple architecture of a house or simple flat 
of a common man. It contains living room, bed rooms, kitchen 
and bathrooms. An architect has to design the house in 
such a manner that all the corners of the house receive 
sunlight and air. It is desirable to receive maximum sunlight 
in bathrooms and kitchen sink since the usage of water is 
here hence the direction of bathrooms should be towards 
East, South East, South or South West. For the house to 
be more airy the doors and windows should be facing towards 
East and West since winds flow easterly and westerly in our 
country Bharat. No auspiciousness or inauspiciousness should 
be attached to any direction, Auspiciousness and 
inauspiciousness are the words attached to religion not 
science. Fate of native does not change by changing any 
direction. A student will not study better if he changes his 
- 224 -
direction. It is the combination of planets in his horoscope 
which will ensure the inclination of study, intelligence level 
and the marks obtained by him. 
Yantra: Meaning ofYantra is machine. It is believed Yantras 
being a machine can promise which is not promised in 
Horoscope whereas experience has shown what is written 
in the destiny remains unchanged. 
Fasting: Fasting a day in a month is good for health. 
Mantras: Mantras are to be adopted as a remedial measure 
only in such cases where the Dasa of a particular planet is 
bad. The Mantras for various planets are given in the following

Precious Stones: Precious stones are adopted as a remedial 
measure for enhancing the strength of a planet which is 
good. 
Now it is amply clear to the reader as well as the astrologer 
the efficacy of remedial measures which can act as double 
edged sword if not used properly. Therefore an able astrologer 
should recommend the Mantra & the Stone only for the 
limited use of happening of the event or preventing the 
happening of an event. The astrologer should suggest the 
native to stop the use of Stone once the event is over. 
- 226 -
One representative example is hereby given to facilitate the 
idea enuntiated above. The combination for getting a job is 
2,6, 10,11 &an astrologer has recommended the use of Stone 
for getting the job after observing the planet (whose stone 
is recommended) is signifying 6,1 0. The Stone should be 
discontinued once he gets the job. He should recommend 
the discontinuance of the Stone immediately otherwise 
continuous use will create obstacles for the native to get 
married because the combination for no marriage is 1, 6, 10.

Weight of the precious stone 
Weight of the precious stone to be worn is derived at with 
the help of "Numerology." 
For the Natives born on 1 ,2,4,7 dates of the month weight 
of the precious stone so chosen should be 4114 or 7114. 
:=or the Natives born on 4,5,8 dates of the month weight of 
the precious stone so chosen should be 4114, 5114 or 8114. 
For the Natives born on 3,6,9 dates of the month weight of 
the precious stone so chosen should be 3114, 61!4 or 91!4. 
There is an exception for the diamond. Diamond can be 
worn from 25 cents onwards. 
Metal forthe ring 
Sun and Jupiter signify gold whereas Moon signify silver and 
Mars signifies copper. The best metal to hold precious stone 
is gold. Silver holds precious stone for not more than a year 
after which the stone becomes loose and there is possibility 
of loosing the stone thereby. Normally people avoid to wear 
stones in copper as it does not give good look. 
Astrologically if Sun or Jupiter are good it is advised to wear 
stone recommended in gold. If Moon is good native should 
wear recommended stone in silver. If Mars is good precious 
stone should be worn in copper. If neither of the planets 
Sun, Jupiter, Moon and Mars are good native should wear 
precious stone in "Panchdhatu".

CHAPTER-17 
TWIN BIRTHS 
Astronomically there is not much difference in the position 
of planets in case of "Twin Births" whereas we find there is 
difference of pattern of lives of th~ Natives although we 
have examples in which there have been similarity in the 
most of the events of life e.g. "Waugh brothers" of Australian 
cricket team. 
During the birth of Twins there is only difference of few 
minutes in the time of birth. Therefore normally the Ascendent 
remains the same. According to Nadi Astrology following 
differences may be there in the Horoscopes of the Twins : 
1) Difference in the longitude of the Ascendent & hence 
difference in the Cuspal Sub lord of the Ascendent & 
Cuspal Sub lord of other houses. 
2) Difference in the position of Planets in the Houses in 
the "Nirayana Bahva Chalit". 
3) Difference in the Sign lords of the Houses in the "Nirayana 
Bhava Chalit". 
The above differences results into different pattern of lives 
of the Twins. Their Longevity, Marriage, Child Birth, Time of 
purchase of property & vehicles etc. will change because 
the Houses signified by the Planets will change due to the 
differences discussed above. 
Following are the Horoscopes of Female Twins born with a 
difference of 7 minutes. 
In both the Horoscopes you will observe three differences 
discussed above. 
- 229 . 
1) There is difference in the longitude of the Ascendent in 
both the Horoscopes. The Cuspal Sub lord of "Ch17 A" 
is Jupiter and that of "Ch17 B" is Saturn. There is 
difference in cuspal sub lords of other Houses too. 
2) There is no change in position of planets in the Nirayana 
Bhav Chalit chart of the twins. 
3) There is difference of the Signs falling on the Houses. 
In the Nirayana Bhav Chalit Chart of the elder sister 
Aries and Libra signs are absent. Virgo sign is repeated 
in the Ascendant and second House whereas Pisces 
sign is repeated in the seventh and eighth House. 
Due to these differences the Houses signified by the Planets 
are different to each other. The events of the lives of the 
Twins were different. 
The native of the Horoscope "Ch 17 B" got married in the 
DBA of Mars - Mercury - Venus. The native of the Horoscope 
"Ch 17 A " got married in Mars - Rahu - Venus. Readers can 
understand that Mercury of the younger sister is good for 
marriage whereas Mercury of the elder sister is neutral for 
the marriage therefore marriage of elder sister was delayed. 
In the Horoscopes ofthe twins Sun, Moon, Rahu and Saturn 
are desired to give similar results since the Houses signified 
in all the planets are the same. 
Readers can observe other planets in the Horoscope and 
find the events of life of both the twins and see the difference 
between the events.

Late Prime Minister Indira Gandhi was the only child of the 
First Prime Minister of our country Pt. Jawahar Lal Nehru. 
She fell in love with a Parasi, Feroz Gandhi who was not 
liked by Pandit j i. She got married to him in 1942 in DBA of 
Rahu - Saturn - Mercury. Mark the presence of 5,8, 12 in 
Rahu and Mercury. She was arrested in Rahu - Saturn -
Ketu and was imprisoned for few months. It was a political 
confinement. Mark the presence of 2,3,8 in Rahu and Ketu. 
She was strong willed, a lioness and strongest administrator 
India has ever seen. Ascendant is political sign Leo and the 
lord signifies 6,7, 12 in Nakshatra and Sub lord. Planet signifies 
1 ,4. All this suggests that she should be highly aggressive 
and cold at heart. Mars is also in the Ascendant which signifies 
5,10 strong will power and determination and 1 ,4, 10 cold at 
heart. Six planets Ketu, Venus, Mars, Rahu, Jupiter and 
Mercury signify 5,1 0. She was stubborn and a born leader. 
From the Dasa of Mars in the very childhood she was the 
leader of "Monkey brigade". 
After holding key positions in social organization she became 
president of youth congress in 1956 in the DBA of Jupiter -
Jupiter - Mars. In 1964 she was taken into Cabinet by Late 
Prime Minister La I Bahadur Shastri as Information broadcasting 
minister in the DBA of Jupiter - Venus - Saturn. She took 
major decisions in the ministry. Mark the presence of 5,10 in 
Venus and Jupiter. 
Lal Bahadur Shastri suddenly died of a heart attack in 1966. 
The contenders for Prime Minister's post could not agree 
among themselves and therefore felt that Indira Gandhi would 
be the best for the position as a Prime Minister. Hence, she 
became the Prime Minister in 1966 in Jupiter- Moon- Saturn, 
till India held the next elections in 1967. She won that 
election- in 1967 in Jupiter- Mars- Rahu, she became the 
- 248 -
first woman ever elected to lead a democracy. In 1971, 
Gandhi was re-elected by campaigning with the slogan "Garibi 
Hatao" (Abolish Poverty) in the DBA of Saturn - Saturn -
Mercury. Astrologically Saturn is better than Jupiter and the 
Dasa of Indira Gandhi also changed. 
On the 3rd of December 1971, in Saturn - Saturn -Venus 
period of Mrs. Gandhi Pakistani Air Force (PAF) struck a 
number of Indian airfields in northern India. By midnight, 
India was officially at war with Pakistan. Two weeks later, 
the war was over. The Indian Army had overrun erstwhile 
East Pakistan (Bangladesh) and taken 93,000 prisoners of 
war. It was one of the swiftest military campaigns in recent 
history. 
From 1973 downfall of Mrs. Gandhi started in the Bhukti of 
Mercury. In June 1975, the High Court of Allahabad found 
her guilty of using illegal practices during the last election 
campaign, and ordered her to vacate her seat. There were 
demands for her resignation. Mrs. Gandhi's response was to 
declare a state of emergency in the DBA of Saturn - Mercury 
- Rahu. Rahu and Mercury both signify 5,10. In early 1977, 
she removed the emergency and called for fresh elections in 
the DBA of Saturn - Ketu - Saturn. Saturn in the Horoscope 
does not signify 5, 10. Her Congress party lost badly at the 
polls in Saturn -Venus- Venus. Venus signifies 5,8,10. Many 
people declared that she was a spent force; but, three 
years later, she was to return as Prime Minister of India in 
Saturn - Venus - Saturn. Saturn signifies 4,11 and Venus 
signifies 8,10,11. 
Her government was confronted with serious challenges to 
its ability to maintain law and order as conflicts between 
religious and ethnic groups broke out in different parts of 
the country. After the army had taken over the Golden Temple 
in Amritsar, the chief shrine of the Sikhs, which had been 
held as an armed camp by a group of militant Sikhs, she 
. 249 -
became the target of Sikh anger and on 31 October 1984 
she was assassinated by her own Sikh bodyguard in the DBA 
of Saturn - Rahu - Jupiter. Rahu and Jupiter signify 5,10. 
Jupiter also signify 8,12 and Saturn signifies 6,12 and 2,7 
along with 4. Pluto in the Horoscope of Mrs. Gandhi signifies

ASSASINS & ASSASSINATIONS 
Father of the nation "Mahatma Gandhi" had very illustrious 
young age when he went to South Africa to study Barrister­
at-Law. After seeing the life of Indians in South Africa he 
took up the cause of Indians against the government. He 
returned to India during the first quarter of this century to 
take up the cause of freedom struggle from colonial rule of 
British . He achieved the freedom for India through peaceful 
& Non-Violent means & got the title Father of the Nation. 
He was assasinated by his own countryman "Nathu Ram 
Vinayak Godse" on 30th January 1948 while going for Pooja 
in Birla Bhawan. DBA of Jupiter-Venus-Sun combination 
signifying Houses 1,6,7,8,12, 4 & Badhak was in operation 
in the Horoscope of Mahatma Gandhi at that time. However 
there is very thin line of demarcation between murder & 
Assassination the relevent houses for Assassination are 5,1 0 
. Since the

which would be created after his Death in his position.

intensity of anything be it a positive or negative. In addition

Assassination of Rajiv Gandhi, Indira Gandhi & others readers 
may refer the above combinations.

house also signify Astrology.

with 8,12 houses. The Horoscope ( H1) is of one of my
